# Plan: Replace Static YAML Lookups with Dynamic OMOP DB Queries

## Context

`unit_normalizer.py` uses `ucum_mappings.yaml` (297 lines) for:
1. **Unit normalization**: clinical unit text → UCUM code + OMOP unit_concept_id
2. **Categorical value mapping**: qualifier words ("positive") → OMOP concept ID
3. **Ordinal scale grading**: ECOG/Karnofsky/NYHA/ASA grades → OMOP concept IDs

This is brittle — misspellings, unseen aliases, or new scales break silently. The OMOP vocabulary DB (already connected via `OMOP_VOCAB_URL`) contains all this data. Replace YAML with dynamic DB queries. No YAML fallback.

---

## Changes

### 1. Add 3 DB lookup functions to `omop_mapper.py`

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py`

```python
def _lookup_ucum_unit(engine, unit_text: str) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    """Query concept+concept_synonym where vocabulary_id='UCUM'.
    Returns (ucum_code, omop_concept_id) or (None, None)."""

def _lookup_value_concept(engine, value_text: str) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    """Query concept where domain_id='Meas Value' for SNOMED qualifiers.
    Returns (normalized_text, omop_concept_id) or (None, None)."""

def _lookup_ordinal_concept(engine, scale_name: str, grade: str) -> int | None:
    """Query concept+concept_synonym for scale+grade combo.
    Returns omop_concept_id or None."""
```

All use `_get_omop_engine()` (existing lazy singleton). All are synchronous (called from sync callers or wrapped in `asyncio.to_thread`).

### 2. Rewrite `unit_normalizer.py` — DB-only, no YAML

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/unit_normalizer.py`

- Delete `_load_ucum_mappings()` YAML loader
- Delete `_load_ordinal_scales()` YAML loader
- Delete `_CONFIG_PATH` reference to `ucum_mappings.yaml`
- Remove `import yaml`

**`normalize_unit()`**: Use `@lru_cache(maxsize=512)` wrapping `_lookup_ucum_unit()`.

**`normalize_value()`**: Use `@lru_cache(maxsize=128)` wrapping `_lookup_value_concept()`.

**`_match_ordinal_scale()`**: Keep as-is but use a Python dict constant instead of YAML:
```python
# Clinical knowledge: which names refer to which scale (not in OMOP)
ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES: dict[str, str] = {
    "ecog": "ecog", "ecog ps": "ecog", "ecog performance status": "ecog",
    "who performance status": "ecog", "zubrod score": "ecog",
    "karnofsky": "karnofsky", "kps": "karnofsky",
    "karnofsky performance status": "karnofsky",
    "nyha": "nyha", "nyha class": "nyha",
    "nyha functional class": "nyha", "new york heart association": "nyha",
    "asa": "asa", "asa physical status": "asa", "asa ps": "asa",
    "asa class": "asa", "asa classification": "asa", "asa score": "asa",
}
```

**`normalize_ordinal_value()`**: Resolve grade→concept_id via `_lookup_ordinal_concept()` instead of YAML values dict. Still returns `(value_concept_id, 8527)`.

**`propose_ordinal_mappings()`**: Delete or simplify — no YAML to scan for missing entries anymore.

### 3. Delete `ucum_mappings.yaml`

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/config/ucum_mappings.yaml`

Delete entirely. No fallback needed — OMOP DB is the single source of truth.

### 4. Update callers

**`structure_builder.py`** (line 30): No change needed — it imports `normalize_unit`, `normalize_value`, `normalize_ordinal_value` which keep the same signatures.

**`ground.py`** (line 51): No change needed — it imports `normalize_value` which keeps the same signature.

```
     1→"""OMOP vocabulary mapper: look up standard OMOP concept_ids for medical entities.
     2→
     3→Queries Athena vocabulary tables (concept, concept_synonym) in a dedicated
     4→OMOP vocabulary database. Requires OMOP_VOCAB_URL environment variable
     5→pointing to a Postgres instance with loaded Athena vocabulary data.
     6→
     7→This tool provides the OMOP grounding leg of the dual-grounding pipeline:
     8→- TerminologyRouter provides UMLS/SNOMED/RxNorm/ICD-10/LOINC/HPO codes
     9→- OmopMapper provides OMOP standard concept_ids for CIRCE export and CDM joins
    10→
    11→Connection: Uses a dedicated SQLAlchemy engine created from OMOP_VOCAB_URL.
    12→This is separate from the main app database (DATABASE_URL). If OMOP_VOCAB_URL
    13→is not set, lookup_omop_concept raises RuntimeError — callers must handle this
    14→explicitly rather than silently receiving empty results.
    15→
    16→All DB I/O is synchronous SQLAlchemy wrapped in run_in_executor for async
    17→compatibility.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import asyncio
    23→import logging
    24→import os
    25→from difflib import SequenceMatcher
    26→from typing import Any
    27→
    28→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    29→from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
    30→
    31→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    32→
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→# Configuration
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE: float = 0.3
    38→"""Minimum fuzzy-match score to accept a candidate. Below this, return None."""
    39→
    40→OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES: int = 50
    41→"""Maximum number of rows to fetch from each SQL query (LIMIT clause)."""
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Dedicated OMOP vocabulary engine (lazy singleton)
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→_omop_engine: Any = None
    48→
    49→
    50→def _get_omop_engine() -> Any:
    51→    """Return the OMOP vocabulary SQLAlchemy engine.
    52→
    53→    Creates a dedicated engine from OMOP_VOCAB_URL on first call.
    54→    Raises RuntimeError if the env var is not set — callers must
    55→    handle this rather than silently degrading.
    56→    """
    57→    global _omop_engine  # noqa: PLW0603
    58→    if _omop_engine is not None:
    59→        return _omop_engine
    60→
    61→    omop_url = os.getenv("OMOP_VOCAB_URL")
    62→    if not omop_url:
    63→        raise RuntimeError(
    64→            "OMOP_VOCAB_URL environment variable is not set. "
    65→            "Set it to the OMOP vocabulary Postgres connection string "
    66→            "(e.g. postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5433/omop_vocab) "
    67→            "or start the omop-vocab container: "
    68→            "docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml --profile omop up -d"
    69→        )
    70→
    71→    _omop_engine = create_engine(omop_url, pool_pre_ping=True)
    72→    logger.info("OMOP vocabulary engine created: %s", omop_url.split("@")[-1])
    73→    return _omop_engine
    74→
    75→
    76→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77→# Entity type -> OMOP domain mapping
    78→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    79→
    80→ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN: dict[str, str] = {
    81→    "Condition": "Condition",
    82→    "Medication": "Drug",
    83→    "Lab_Value": "Measurement",
    84→    "Procedure": "Procedure",
    85→    "Demographic": "Observation",
    86→}
    87→"""Map pipeline entity types to OMOP CDM domain_id values (legacy single-domain).
    88→
    89→Demographic is a catch-all routed to Observation. Entity types not present
    90→here will fall back to Observation as well.
    91→"""
    92→
    93→ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAINS: dict[str, list[str]] = {
    94→    "Condition": ["Condition", "Observation"],
    95→    "Medication": ["Drug"],
    96→    "Lab_Value": ["Measurement", "Observation"],
    97→    "Procedure": ["Procedure", "Observation"],
    98→    "Demographic": ["Observation", "Measurement"],
    99→}
   100→"""Map pipeline entity types to OMOP CDM domain_id values (multi-domain).
   101→
   102→Primary domain is first in the list. Fallback domains follow.
   103→Candidates from the primary domain receive a small scoring bonus.
   104→"""
   105→
   106→PRIMARY_DOMAIN_BONUS: float = 0.05
   107→"""Small scoring bonus applied to candidates from the primary (first) domain."""
   108→
   109→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   110→# Result schema
   111→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   112→
   113→
   114→class OmopLookupResult(BaseModel):
   115→    """Result of an OMOP vocabulary lookup for a single entity.
   116→
   117→    Attributes:
   118→        omop_concept_id: Matched OMOP concept_id as a string, or None.
   119→        omop_concept_name: Matched concept_name from the vocabulary, or None.
   120→        omop_vocabulary_id: Vocabulary source of the match (e.g. SNOMED, RxNorm).
   121→        omop_domain_id: OMOP domain_id of the matched concept.
   122→        match_score: Fuzzy similarity score between 0.0 and 1.0.
   123→        match_method: How the match was found: "concept_name" or "synonym".
   124→    """
   125→
   126→    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   127→        default=None,
   128→        description=("Matched OMOP concept_id as a string, or None if no match."),
   129→    )
   130→    omop_concept_name: str | None = Field(
   131→        default=None,
   132→        description="Matched concept_name from the OMOP vocabulary.",
   133→    )
   134→    omop_vocabulary_id: str | None = Field(
   135→        default=None,
   136→        description=("Vocabulary source of the match (e.g. 'SNOMED', 'RxNorm')."),
   137→    )
   138→    omop_domain_id: str | None = Field(
   139→        default=None,
   140→        description="OMOP domain_id of the matched concept.",
   141→    )
   142→    match_score: float = Field(
   143→        default=0.0,
   144→        ge=0.0,
   145→        le=1.0,
   146→        description="Fuzzy similarity score between 0.0 and 1.0.",
   147→    )
   148→    match_method: str = Field(
   149→        default="",
   150→        description='How the match was found: "concept_name" or "synonym".',
   151→    )
   152→
   153→
   154→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   155→# Internal helpers
   156→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   157→
   158→
   159→def _score_candidates(
   160→    entity_text: str, candidates: list[dict[str, Any]]
   161→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   162→    """Score and sort candidates by fuzzy string similarity to *entity_text*.
   163→
   164→    Uses ``difflib.SequenceMatcher`` ratio as the base score, with a bonus
   165→    for exact case-insensitive substring containment.
   166→
   167→    Args:
   168→        entity_text: The original entity text to match against.
   169→        candidates: List of candidate dicts, each with at least a
   170→            ``"match_text"`` key containing the string to compare.
   171→
   172→    Returns:
   173→        The same candidate dicts augmented with a ``"score"`` key, sorted
   174→        in descending order of score.
   175→    """
   176→    entity_lower = entity_text.lower().strip()
   177→
   178→    for candidate in candidates:
   179→        match_text = candidate.get("match_text", "").lower().strip()
   180→
   181→        # Base score: SequenceMatcher ratio
   182→        base_score = SequenceMatcher(None, entity_lower, match_text).ratio()
   183→
   184→        # Bonus for exact substring containment (either direction)
   185→        bonus = 0.0
   186→        if entity_lower in match_text or match_text in entity_lower:
   187→            bonus = 0.15
   188→
   189→        # Bonus for exact match
   190→        if entity_lower == match_text:
   191→            bonus = 0.25
   192→
   193→        candidate["score"] = min(base_score + bonus, 1.0)
   194→
   195→    # Sort by score desc, then by length similarity (prefer match_text
   196→    # closest in length to entity_text) as tiebreaker.
   197→    entity_len = len(entity_lower)
   198→
   199→    def _sort_key(c: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[float, int]:
   200→        match_len = len(c.get("match_text", "").strip())
   201→        return (c["score"], -abs(match_len - entity_len))
   202→
   203→    candidates.sort(key=_sort_key, reverse=True)
   204→    return candidates
   205→
   206→
   207→def _get_domain_filter(entity_type: str) -> str:
   208→    """Resolve entity type to an OMOP domain_id filter value.
   209→
   210→    Falls back to ``"Observation"`` for unknown entity types.
   211→
   212→    Args:
   213→        entity_type: Pipeline entity type (e.g. "Condition", "Medication").
   214→
   215→    Returns:
   216→        OMOP domain_id string.
   217→    """
   218→    domain = ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN.get(entity_type, "Observation")
   219→    if entity_type not in ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN:
   220→        logger.info(
   221→            "Entity type '%s' not in OMOP domain map — defaulting to '%s'",
   222→            entity_type,
   223→            domain,
   224→        )
   225→    return domain
   226→
   227→
   228→def _get_domain_filters(entity_type: str) -> list[str]:
   229→    """Resolve entity type to a list of OMOP domain_id filter values.
   230→
   231→    Returns primary + fallback domains. The first domain in the list
   232→    is the primary domain (candidates from it receive a scoring bonus).
   233→    Falls back to ``["Observation"]`` for unknown entity types.
   234→
   235→    Args:
   236→        entity_type: Pipeline entity type (e.g. "Condition", "Medication").
   237→
   238→    Returns:
   239→        List of OMOP domain_id strings, primary first.
   240→    """
   241→    domains = ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAINS.get(entity_type)
   242→    if domains is not None:
   243→        return domains
   244→    logger.info(
   245→        "Entity type '%s' not in OMOP multi-domain map — defaulting to ['Observation']",
   246→        entity_type,
   247→    )
   248→    return ["Observation"]
   249→
   250→
   251→def _query_concept_table(
   252→    engine: Any, entity_text: str, domain_ids: list[str]
   253→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   254→    """Query concept table for standard concepts matching *entity_text*.
   255→
   256→    Args:
   257→        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.
   258→        entity_text: Text to search for (used in ILIKE pattern).
   259→        domain_ids: List of OMOP domain_id values to filter on.
   260→
   261→    Returns:
   262→        List of candidate dicts with keys: concept_id, concept_name,
   263→        domain_id, vocabulary_id, match_text, match_method.
   264→    """
   265→    # Build parameterized IN clause for domain_ids
   266→    domain_params = {f"d{i}": d for i, d in enumerate(domain_ids)}
   267→    domain_placeholders = ", ".join(f":d{i}" for i in range(len(domain_ids)))
   268→
   269→    sql = text(
   270→        "SELECT concept_id, concept_name, domain_id, vocabulary_id "
   271→        "FROM concept "
   272→        "WHERE standard_concept = 'S' "
   273→        f"  AND domain_id IN ({domain_placeholders}) "
   274→        "  AND concept_name ILIKE :pattern "
   275→        "ORDER BY length(concept_name) ASC "
   276→        "LIMIT :max_candidates"
   277→    )
   278→    pattern = f"%{entity_text}%"
   279→    params = {
   280→        **domain_params,
   281→        "pattern": pattern,
   282→        "max_candidates": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,
   283→    }
   284→    with engine.connect() as conn:
   285→        rows = conn.execute(sql, params).fetchall()
   286→
   287→    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   288→    for row in rows:
   289→        candidates.append(
   290→            {
   291→                "concept_id": str(row[0]),
   292→                "concept_name": row[1],
   293→                "domain_id": row[2],
   294→                "vocabulary_id": row[3],
   295→                "match_text": row[1],
   296→                "match_method": "concept_name",
   297→            }
   298→        )
   299→    return candidates
   300→
   301→
   302→def _query_synonym_table(
   303→    engine: Any,
   304→    entity_text: str,
   305→    domain_ids: list[str],
   306→    *,
   307→    skip_domain_filter: bool = False,
   308→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   309→    """Query concept_synonym joined with concept for synonym matches.
   310→
   311→    Args:
   312→        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.
   313→        entity_text: Text to search for (used in ILIKE pattern).
   314→        domain_ids: List of OMOP domain_id values to filter on.
   315→        skip_domain_filter: If True, search across ALL domains (used for
   316→            acronym lookups where the domain may be ambiguous).
   317→
   318→    Returns:
   319→        List of candidate dicts with keys: concept_id, concept_name,
   320→        domain_id, vocabulary_id, match_text, match_method.
   321→    """
   322→    pattern = f"%{entity_text}%"
   323→
   324→    if skip_domain_filter:
   325→        sql = text(
   326→            "SELECT c.concept_id, c.concept_name, c.domain_id, "
   327→            "       c.vocabulary_id, s.concept_synonym_name "
   328→            "FROM concept_synonym s "
   329→            "JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id "
   330→            "WHERE c.standard_concept = 'S' "
   331→            "  AND s.concept_synonym_name ILIKE :pattern "
   332→            "ORDER BY length(s.concept_synonym_name) ASC "
   333→            "LIMIT :max_candidates"
   334→        )
   335→        params: dict[str, Any] = {
   336→            "pattern": pattern,
   337→            "max_candidates": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,
   338→        }
   339→    else:
   340→        domain_params = {f"d{i}": d for i, d in enumerate(domain_ids)}
   341→        domain_placeholders = ", ".join(f":d{i}" for i in range(len(domain_ids)))
   342→        sql = text(
   343→            "SELECT c.concept_id, c.concept_name, c.domain_id, "
   344→            "       c.vocabulary_id, s.concept_synonym_name "
   345→            "FROM concept_synonym s "
   346→            "JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id "
   347→            "WHERE c.standard_concept = 'S' "
   348→            f"  AND c.domain_id IN ({domain_placeholders}) "
   349→            "  AND s.concept_synonym_name ILIKE :pattern "
   350→            "ORDER BY length(s.concept_synonym_name) ASC "
   351→            "LIMIT :max_candidates"
   352→        )
   353→        params = {
   354→            **domain_params,
   355→            "pattern": pattern,
   356→            "max_candidates": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,
   357→        }
   358→
   359→    with engine.connect() as conn:
   360→        rows = conn.execute(sql, params).fetchall()
   361→
   362→    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   363→    for row in rows:
   364→        candidates.append(
   365→            {
   366→                "concept_id": str(row[0]),
   367→                "concept_name": row[1],
   368→                "domain_id": row[2],
   369→                "vocabulary_id": row[3],
   370→                "match_text": row[4],  # synonym name used for scoring
   371→                "match_method": "synonym",
   372→            }
   373→        )
   374→    return candidates
   375→
   376→
   377→def _sync_lookup(
   378→    entity_text: str,
   379→    domain_ids: list[str],
   380→    *,
   381→    is_acronym: bool = False,
   382→) -> OmopLookupResult:
   383→    """Synchronous OMOP lookup — runs inside run_in_executor.
   384→
   385→    Queries both concept and concept_synonym tables, deduplicates
   386→    candidates by concept_id (preferring concept_name match), scores them,
   387→    and returns the best match above the threshold.
   388→
   389→    Args:
   390→        entity_text: The entity text to look up.
   391→        domain_ids: List of OMOP domain_id values to filter on.
   392→            The first domain is considered "primary" and gets a scoring bonus.
   393→        is_acronym: If True, skip domain filter for synonym search
   394→            (acronyms may appear in any OMOP domain).
   395→
   396→    Returns:
   397→        OmopLookupResult with best match, or empty result if nothing found
   398→        or all candidates score below OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE.
   399→
   400→    Raises:
   401→        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not configured.
   402→        Exception: Database errors are propagated, not swallowed.
   403→    """
   404→    engine = _get_omop_engine()
   405→
   406→    concept_candidates = _query_concept_table(engine, entity_text, domain_ids)
   407→    synonym_candidates = _query_synonym_table(
   408→        engine,
   409→        entity_text,
   410→        domain_ids,
   411→        skip_domain_filter=is_acronym,
   412→    )
   413→
   414→    # Deduplicate by concept_id — prefer concept_name match over synonym
   415→    seen: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   416→    for candidate in concept_candidates:
   417→        seen[candidate["concept_id"]] = candidate
   418→    for candidate in synonym_candidates:
   419→        if candidate["concept_id"] not in seen:
   420→            seen[candidate["concept_id"]] = candidate
   421→
   422→    all_candidates = list(seen.values())
   423→
   424→    if not all_candidates:
   425→        logger.debug(
   426→            "No OMOP candidates found for '%s' in domains %s",
   427→            entity_text[:50],
   428→            domain_ids,
   429→        )
   430→        return OmopLookupResult()
   431→
   432→    # Score and sort
   433→    scored = _score_candidates(entity_text, all_candidates)
   434→
   435→    # Apply primary domain bonus
   436→    primary_domain = domain_ids[0] if domain_ids else None
   437→    if primary_domain:
   438→        for candidate in scored:
   439→            if candidate.get("domain_id") == primary_domain:
   440→                candidate["score"] = min(candidate["score"] + PRIMARY_DOMAIN_BONUS, 1.0)
   441→        # Re-sort after bonus
   442→        scored.sort(key=lambda c: c["score"], reverse=True)
   443→
   444→    best = scored[0]
   445→
   446→    if best["score"] < OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE:
   447→        logger.debug(
   448→            "Best OMOP candidate for '%s' scored %.3f "
   449→            "(below threshold %.2f) — skipping",
   450→            entity_text[:50],
   451→            best["score"],
   452→            OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE,
   453→        )
   454→        return OmopLookupResult()
   455→
   456→    logger.info(
   457→        "OMOP match for '%s': concept_id=%s, name='%s', score=%.3f, method=%s",
   458→        entity_text[:50],
   459→        best["concept_id"],
   460→        best["concept_name"][:60],
   461→        best["score"],
   462→        best["match_method"],
   463→    )
   464→
   465→    return OmopLookupResult(
   466→        omop_concept_id=best["concept_id"],
   467→        omop_concept_name=best["concept_name"],
   468→        omop_vocabulary_id=best["vocabulary_id"],
   469→        omop_domain_id=best["domain_id"],
   470→        match_score=best["score"],
   471→        match_method=best["match_method"],
   472→    )
   473→
   474→
   475→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   476→# Public async API
   477→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   478→
   479→
   480→async def lookup_omop_concept(
   481→    entity_text: str,
   482→    entity_type: str,
   483→    *,
   484→    is_acronym: bool = False,
   485→) -> OmopLookupResult:
   486→    """Look up the best OMOP standard concept for a medical entity.
   487→
   488→    Maps the entity type to OMOP domain filters (primary + fallback),
   489→    queries the concept and concept_synonym tables in the OMOP vocabulary
   490→    database, scores candidates with fuzzy string matching, and returns
   491→    the best result above the minimum similarity threshold.
   492→
   493→    The synchronous database I/O is executed in a thread-pool executor to
   494→    avoid blocking the async event loop.
   495→
   496→    Requires OMOP_VOCAB_URL to be set. Raises RuntimeError if not configured.
   497→
   498→    Args:
   499→        entity_text: The medical entity text to look up (e.g. "metformin",
   500→            "type 2 diabetes mellitus", "hemoglobin A1c").
   501→        entity_type: Pipeline entity type used to determine the OMOP domain
   502→            filter. One of "Condition", "Medication", "Lab_Value",
   503→            "Procedure", "Demographic". Unknown types fall back to
   504→            "Observation".
   505→        is_acronym: If True, skip domain filter for synonym search
   506→            to find acronyms across all OMOP domains.
   507→
   508→    Returns:
   509→        OmopLookupResult with the best-matching OMOP concept, or an empty
   510→        result if no match is found above the similarity threshold.
   511→
   512→    Raises:
   513→        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not set.
   514→    """
   515→    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():
   516→        raise ValueError("Empty entity_text passed to lookup_omop_concept")
   517→
   518→    domain_ids = _get_domain_filters(entity_type)
   519→
   520→    logger.debug(
   521→        "OMOP lookup: entity='%s', type='%s', domains=%s, acronym=%s",
   522→        entity_text[:50],
   523→        entity_type,
   524→        domain_ids,
   525→        is_acronym,
   526→    )
   527→
   528→    result = await asyncio.to_thread(
   529→        _sync_lookup, entity_text, domain_ids, is_acronym=is_acronym
   530→    )
   531→
   532→    return result
   533→
```

```
     1→"""Unit and value normalizer: UCUM lookup with OMOP concept ID resolution.
     2→
     3→Phase 3 (Gap 7): Deterministic YAML-based lookup mapping common clinical
     4→trial units to UCUM codes and OMOP unit_concept_id values, plus optional
     5→categorical value normalization (e.g. "positive" -> SNOMED qualifier concept).
     6→
     7→Phase 3b: Ordinal scale normalization (ECOG, Karnofsky, NYHA, etc.) with
     8→agent-assisted concept resolution.  Recognized ordinal entities get
     9→unit_concept_id=8527 ({score}); value_concept_id comes from YAML config
    10→(populated incrementally via agent/approval workflow).
    11→
    12→No LLM calls — pure static lookup with alias expansion and case-insensitive
    13→matching.  Returns (None, None) for unrecognized inputs; never raises.
    14→"""
    15→
    16→from __future__ import annotations
    17→
    18→import logging
    19→import re
    20→from functools import lru_cache
    21→from pathlib import Path
    22→from typing import Any
    23→
    24→import yaml
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config" / "ucum_mappings.yaml"
    29→
    30→_ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE = re.compile(
    31→    r"^(?:grade|stage|class|score|level)\s*",
    32→    re.IGNORECASE,
    33→)
    34→
    35→
    36→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
    37→def _load_ucum_mappings() -> tuple[dict[str, tuple[str, int]], dict[str, int]]:
    38→    """Load and index the UCUM mappings YAML (cached after first call).
    39→
    40→    Returns:
    41→        Tuple of (unit_lookup, value_lookup) where:
    42→        - unit_lookup: lowercased alias/canonical -> (canonical, omop_id)
    43→        - value_lookup: lowercased value text -> omop_value_concept_id
    44→    """
    45→    with open(_CONFIG_PATH) as f:
    46→        data: dict[str, Any] = yaml.safe_load(f)
    47→
    48→    unit_lookup: dict[str, tuple[str, int]] = {}
    49→    for entry in data.get("units", []):
    50→        canonical: str = entry["canonical"]
    51→        omop_id: int = entry["omop_unit_concept_id"]
    52→        # Index canonical form
    53→        unit_lookup[canonical.strip().lower()] = (canonical, omop_id)
    54→        # Index all aliases
    55→        for alias in entry.get("aliases", []):
    56→            unit_lookup[alias.strip().lower()] = (canonical, omop_id)
    57→
    58→    value_lookup: dict[str, int] = {}
    59→    for text, concept_id in data.get("value_mappings", {}).items():
    60→        value_lookup[str(text).strip().lower()] = int(concept_id)
    61→
    62→    return unit_lookup, value_lookup
    63→
    64→
    65→def normalize_unit(unit_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    66→    """Normalize a unit string to its UCUM code and OMOP unit_concept_id.
    67→
    68→    Args:
    69→        unit_text: Raw unit text (e.g. "mg/dL", "%", "years").
    70→
    71→    Returns:
    72→        Tuple of (ucum_code, omop_unit_concept_id), or (None, None)
    73→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    74→    """
    75→    if unit_text is not None and not isinstance(unit_text, str):
    76→        unit_text = str(unit_text)
    77→    if not unit_text or not unit_text.strip():
    78→        return (None, None)
    79→
    80→    key = unit_text.strip().lower()
    81→    unit_lookup, _ = _load_ucum_mappings()
    82→    result = unit_lookup.get(key)
    83→    if result is not None:
    84→        return result
    85→    return (None, None)
    86→
    87→
    88→def normalize_value(
    89→    value_text: str | None,
    90→) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    91→    """Normalize a categorical value to its SNOMED code and OMOP concept ID.
    92→
    93→    Args:
    94→        value_text: Raw value text (e.g. "positive", "negative", "normal").
    95→
    96→    Returns:
    97→        Tuple of (normalized_text, omop_value_concept_id), or (None, None)
    98→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    99→    """
   100→    if value_text is not None and not isinstance(value_text, str):
   101→        value_text = str(value_text)
   102→    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():
   103→        return (None, None)
   104→
   105→    key = value_text.strip().lower()
   106→    _, value_lookup = _load_ucum_mappings()
   107→    concept_id = value_lookup.get(key)
   108→    if concept_id is not None:
   109→        return (key, concept_id)
   110→    return (None, None)
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Ordinal scale normalization (Phase 3b)
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→
   118→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   119→def _load_ordinal_scales() -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, Any]]:
   120→    """Load ordinal scale definitions from YAML config (cached).
   121→
   122→    Returns:
   123→        Tuple of (alias_to_scale, scale_defs) where:
   124→        - alias_to_scale: lowercased alias -> scale key (e.g. "ecog")
   125→        - scale_defs: raw ordinal_scales dict from YAML
   126→    """
   127→    with open(_CONFIG_PATH) as f:
   128→        data: dict[str, Any] = yaml.safe_load(f)
   129→
   130→    scale_defs: dict[str, Any] = data.get("ordinal_scales", {})
   131→    alias_to_scale: dict[str, str] = {}
   132→    for scale_key, scale_def in scale_defs.items():
   133→        for alias in scale_def.get("entity_aliases", []):
   134→            alias_to_scale[alias.strip().lower()] = scale_key
   135→
   136→    return alias_to_scale, scale_defs
   137→
   138→
   139→def _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text: str) -> str | None:
   140→    """Match entity text to an ordinal scale key.
   141→
   142→    Uses three strategies in order:
   143→    1. Exact alias match (case-insensitive)
   144→    2. Entity text is a substring of an alias
   145→    3. An alias is a substring of entity text
   146→
   147→    Returns:
   148→        Scale key (e.g. "ecog") or None if no match.
   149→    """
   150→    alias_to_scale, _ = _load_ordinal_scales()
   151→    key = entity_text.strip().lower()
   152→
   153→    # Strategy 1: exact alias match
   154→    if key in alias_to_scale:
   155→        return alias_to_scale[key]
   156→
   157→    # Strategy 2 & 3: bidirectional substring containment
   158→    for alias, scale_key in alias_to_scale.items():
   159→        if key in alias or alias in key:
   160→            return scale_key
   161→
   162→    return None
   163→
   164→
   165→def normalize_ordinal_value(
   166→    value_text: str | None,
   167→    entity_text: str | None = None,
   168→) -> tuple[int | None, int | None] | None:
   169→    """Entity-context-aware ordinal value normalization.
   170→
   171→    Returns:
   172→        (value_concept_id, unit_concept_id) if entity matches an ordinal scale.
   173→        value_concept_id is the approved OMOP ID from YAML (or None if not yet
   174→        resolved).  unit_concept_id is always 8527 ({score}) for ordinal scales.
   175→        Returns None (not a tuple) if entity doesn't match any ordinal scale.
   176→    """
   177→    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():
   178→        return None
   179→
   180→    scale_key = _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text)
   181→    if scale_key is None:
   182→        return None
   183→
   184→    _, scale_defs = _load_ordinal_scales()
   185→    scale_def = scale_defs[scale_key]
   186→    unit_concept_id: int | None = scale_def.get("unit_concept_id")
   187→
   188→    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():
   189→        return (None, unit_concept_id)
   190→
   191→    # Normalize value: strip ordinal prefixes, convert "2.0" -> "2"
   192→    cleaned = _ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE.sub("", value_text.strip())
   193→    try:
   194→        numeric = float(cleaned)
   195→        if numeric == int(numeric):
   196→            cleaned = str(int(numeric))
   197→        else:
   198→            cleaned = str(numeric)
   199→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
   200→        cleaned = cleaned.strip()
   201→
   202→    values = scale_def.get("values", {})
   203→    val_entry = values.get(cleaned)
   204→    if val_entry is None:
   205→        # Grade not in the defined values for this scale
   206→        return (None, unit_concept_id)
   207→
   208→    omop_value_cid = val_entry.get("omop_value_concept_id")
   209→    return (omop_value_cid, unit_concept_id)
   210→
   211→
   212→def propose_ordinal_mappings() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   213→    """Scan ordinal_scales config and return entries missing omop_value_concept_id.
   214→
   215→    Returns a list of dicts, each with:
   216→      - scale: scale key (e.g., "ecog")
   217→      - grade: value string (e.g., "2")
   218→      - snomed_code: known SNOMED code (if any)
   219→      - loinc_answer: known LOINC answer code (if any)
   220→      - description: human-readable description
   221→      - status: "needs_resolution"
   222→
   223→    This output can be fed to an LLM agent or OMOP vocabulary lookup to propose
   224→    omop_value_concept_id values.  Once approved, add them to the YAML.
   225→    """
   226→    _, scale_defs = _load_ordinal_scales()
   227→    missing: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   228→
   229→    for scale_key, scale_def in scale_defs.items():
   230→        for grade, val_entry in scale_def.get("values", {}).items():
   231→            if val_entry.get("omop_value_concept_id") is not None:
   232→                continue
   233→            missing.append(
   234→                {
   235→                    "scale": scale_key,
   236→                    "grade": grade,
   237→                    "snomed_code": val_entry.get("snomed_code"),
   238→                    "loinc_answer": val_entry.get("loinc_answer"),
   239→                    "description": val_entry.get("description"),
   240→                    "status": "needs_resolution",
   241→                }
   242→            )
   243→
   244→    return missing
   245→
```

```
     1→"""Tests for grounding fixes across the codebase.
     2→
     3→Covers:
     4→- FieldMappingItem Literal relation validation and legacy operator normalization
     5→- _is_likely_acronym detection logic and allowlist
     6→- TerminologyRouter smart acronym routing (UMLS injection)
     7→- OMOP mapper domain filtering and primary domain scoring bonus
     8→- entity_decompose.jinja2 compound-concept rules in the prompt template
     9→- FieldMappingItem value_concept_id / value_concept_system optional fields
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
    16→
    17→import pydantic
    18→import pytest
    19→

    35→# Helpers

    37→

    39→
    40→_ENTITY_DECOMPOSE_TEMPLATE = _PROMPTS_DIR / "entity_decompose.jinja2"
    41→
    42→
    43→def _make_candidate(
    44→    match_text: str, domain_id: str = "Condition", **extra: object
    45→) -> dict:
    46→    """Build a minimal candidate dict suitable for _score_candidates."""
    47→    base: dict = {
    48→        "concept_id": "99999",
    49→        "concept_name": match_text,
    50→        "domain_id": domain_id,
    51→        "vocabulary_id": "SNOMED",
    52→        "match_text": match_text,
    53→        "match_method": "concept_name",
    54→    }
    55→    base.update(extra)
    56→    return base
    57→

    59→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→# Test 1 — FieldMappingItem rejects invalid relation literal
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→
    63→
    64→class TestRelationLiteralRejectsInvalid:
    65→    """FieldMappingItem.relation must be one of the accepted RelationOperator values."""
    66→
    67→    def test_relation_literal_rejects_invalid(self) -> None:
    68→        """'has_value' is not a valid RelationOperator — raises ValidationError."""
    69→        with pytest.raises(pydantic.ValidationError):
    70→            FieldMappingItem(
    71→                entity="x",
    72→                relation="has_value",
    73→                value=FieldMappingValue(type="standard", value="1"),
    74→            )
    75→
    76→
    77→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→# Test 2 — @field_validator normalizes legacy operators before Literal check
    79→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    80→
    81→
    82→class TestRelationNormalizerMapsLegacy:
    83→    """The @field_validator on FieldMappingItem.relation normalizes legacy operators."""
    84→
    85→    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
    86→        ("legacy_op", "expected"),
    87→        [
    88→            ("has", "contains"),
    89→            ("is", "="),
    90→            ("==", "="),
    91→            ("not", "not_contains"),
    92→            ("range", "within"),
    93→        ],
    94→    )
    95→    def test_normalizer_maps_legacy(self, legacy_op: str, expected: str) -> None:
    96→        """Each legacy operator maps to the canonical frontend operator."""
    97→        item = FieldMappingItem(
    98→            entity="glucose",
    99→            relation=legacy_op,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   100→            value=FieldMappingValue(type="standard", value="100", unit="mg/dL"),
   101→        )
   102→        assert item.relation == expected
   103→

   106→# Test 3 — _is_likely_acronym detection
   107→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   108→
   109→
   110→class TestIsLikelyAcronym:
   111→    """_is_likely_acronym returns True for short all-caps tokens not in allowlist."""
   112→
   113→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("text", ["HTN", "MI", "CKD", "COPD", "DM", "AF"])
   114→    def test_true_for_medical_acronyms(self, text: str) -> None:
   115→        """Common medical acronyms should be detected as acronyms."""
   116→        assert _is_likely_acronym(text) is True
   117→
   118→    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
   119→        "text",
   120→        [
   121→            "BMI",  # short all-caps — detected as acronym (redundant UMLS is OK)
   122→            "AST",  # short all-caps — detected as acronym
   123→        ],
   124→    )
   125→    def test_true_for_short_allcaps(self, text: str) -> None:
   126→        """Short all-caps tokens are acronyms (no allowlist)."""
   127→        assert _is_likely_acronym(text) is True
   128→
   129→    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
   130→        "text",
   131→        [
   132→            "age",  # not all-caps
   133→            "metformin",  # not all-caps, longer
   134→            "body mass index",  # has spaces
   135→            "sex",  # not all-caps
   136→        ],
   137→    )
   138→    def test_false_for_non_acronyms(self, text: str) -> None:
   139→        """Non-acronyms (lowercase, spaces) return False."""
   140→        assert _is_likely_acronym(text) is False

   144→# Test 4 — Acronym routing adds UMLS to the API list
   145→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146→
   147→
   148→class TestAcronymRoutingAddsUmls:
   149→    """TerminologyRouter.route_entity injects 'umls' when entity looks like acronym."""
   150→
   151→    @pytest.fixture()
   152→    def custom_router(self, tmp_path: Path) -> TerminologyRouter:
   153→        """TerminologyRouter with a TestType rule that does NOT include umls."""
   154→        import yaml
   155→
   156→        config = {
   157→            "routing_rules": {
   158→                "TestType": ["icd10"],
   159→            },
   160→            "api_configs": {
   161→                "icd10": {"source": "tooluniverse", "tool_name": "ICD10_search_codes"},
   162→                "umls": {"source": "tooluniverse", "tool_name": "umls_search_concepts"},
   163→            },
   164→        }
   165→        config_path = tmp_path / "routing.yaml"
   166→        config_path.write_text(yaml.dump(config))
   167→        return TerminologyRouter(config_path=config_path)
   168→
   169→    def test_get_apis_for_entity_does_not_include_umls(
   170→        self, custom_router: TerminologyRouter
   171→    ) -> None:
   172→        """Baseline: TestType routing only has icd10, NOT umls."""
   173→        apis = custom_router.get_apis_for_entity("TestType")
   174→        assert apis == ["icd10"]
   175→        assert "umls" not in apis
   176→
   177→    def test_is_likely_acronym_htn_is_true(self) -> None:
   178→        """HTN is recognized as an acronym (prerequisite for routing logic)."""
   179→        assert _is_likely_acronym("HTN") is True
   180→
   181→    async def test_route_entity_calls_query_tooluniverse_with_umls(
   182→        self, custom_router: TerminologyRouter
   183→    ) -> None:
   184→        """route_entity for an acronym calls _query_tooluniverse with 'umls'."""
   185→        with patch.object(
   186→            custom_router,
   187→            "_query_tooluniverse",
   188→            return_value=[],
   189→        ) as mock_query:
   190→            await custom_router.route_entity("HTN", "TestType")
   191→
   192→        # Collect all api_name args passed to _query_tooluniverse
   193→        called_apis = [call.args[0] for call in mock_query.call_args_list]
   194→        assert "umls" in called_apis, (
   195→            f"Expected 'umls' in called APIs for acronym 'HTN', got: {called_apis}"
   196→        )
   197→        assert "icd10" in called_apis

   204→
   205→class TestOmopAcronymDropsDomainFilter:

   211→    def test_acronym_skips_domain_filter_in_synonym_query(
   212→        self,

   214→        mock_synonym: MagicMock,
   215→        mock_engine: MagicMock,

   262→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   263→# Test 7 — Primary domain candidate gets PRIMARY_DOMAIN_BONUS
   264→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   265→
   266→
   267→class TestOmopPrimaryDomainScoredHigher:
   268→    """Candidates in the primary domain receive PRIMARY_DOMAIN_BONUS after scoring."""
   269→
   270→    @patch("protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper._get_omop_engine")
   271→    @patch("protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper._query_synonym_table")
   272→    @patch("protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper._query_concept_table")
   273→    def test_primary_domain_candidate_ranked_higher(
   274→        self,
   275→        mock_concept: MagicMock,
   276→        mock_synonym: MagicMock,
   277→        mock_engine: MagicMock,
   278→    ) -> None:
   279→        """Condition candidate (primary domain) should rank above Observation."""
   280→        mock_engine.return_value = MagicMock()
   281→        mock_synonym.return_value = []
   282→
   283→        # Both candidates have the same match_text; domain differentiates them.
   284→        condition_candidate = _make_candidate(
   285→            "diabetes mellitus",
   286→            domain_id="Condition",
   287→            concept_id="111",
   288→        )
   289→        observation_candidate = _make_candidate(
   290→            "diabetes mellitus",
   291→            domain_id="Observation",
   292→            concept_id="222",
   293→        )
   294→        mock_concept.return_value = [condition_candidate, observation_candidate]
   295→
   296→        result = _sync_lookup("diabetes mellitus", ["Condition", "Observation"])
   297→
   298→        # Primary domain is "Condition" — winning concept_id should be from Condition
   299→        assert result.omop_concept_id == "111", (
   300→            f"Expected Condition concept '111' to win, got: {result.omop_concept_id}"
   301→        )
   302→
   303→    def test_primary_domain_bonus_constant_is_correct(self) -> None:
   304→        """PRIMARY_DOMAIN_BONUS must equal 0.05."""
   305→        assert PRIMARY_DOMAIN_BONUS == pytest.approx(0.05)
   306→
   307→    def test_score_candidates_does_not_apply_domain_bonus(self) -> None:
   308→        """_score_candidates does NOT apply the domain bonus (done in _sync_lookup)."""
   309→        candidates = [
   310→            _make_candidate("diabetes mellitus", domain_id="Condition"),
   311→            _make_candidate("diabetes mellitus", domain_id="Observation"),
   312→        ]
   313→        scored = _score_candidates("diabetes mellitus", candidates)
   314→        # Without the domain bonus, both should have identical scores
   315→        assert scored[0]["score"] == pytest.approx(scored[1]["score"])
   316→
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py",
  "old_string": "async def lookup_omop_concept(",
  "new_string": "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Unit / value / ordinal DB lookups (used by unit_normalizer)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _lookup_ucum_unit(engine: Any, unit_text: str) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Query concept + concept_synonym where vocabulary_id='UCUM'.\n\n    Searches for *unit_text* (case-insensitive) in both concept_name and\n    concept_synonym_name within UCUM vocabulary. Returns the best match.\n\n    Args:\n        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.\n        unit_text: Raw unit string (e.g. \"mg/dL\", \"years\", \"%\").\n\n    Returns:\n        (ucum_code, omop_concept_id) or (None, None) if not found.\n    \"\"\"\n    key = unit_text.strip()\n    if not key:\n        return (None, None)\n\n    # Try exact concept_name match first (fastest path)\n    sql_exact = text(\n        \"SELECT concept_id, concept_name \"\n        \"FROM concept \"\n        \"WHERE vocabulary_id = 'UCUM' \"\n        \"  AND standard_concept = 'S' \"\n        \"  AND LOWER(concept_name) = LOWER(:unit_text) \"\n        \"LIMIT 1\"\n    )\n    with engine.connect() as conn:\n        row = conn.execute(sql_exact, {\"unit_text\": key}).fetchone()\n        if row is not None:\n            return (row[1], int(row[0]))\n\n        # Try synonym match\n        sql_syn = text(\n            \"SELECT c.concept_id, c.concept_name \"\n            \"FROM concept_synonym s \"\n            \"JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id \"\n            \"WHERE c.vocabulary_id = 'UCUM' \"\n            \"  AND c.standard_concept = 'S' \"\n            \"  AND LOWER(s.concept_synonym_name) = LOWER(:unit_text) \"\n            \"LIMIT 1\"\n        )\n        row = conn.execute(sql_syn, {\"unit_text\": key}).fetchone()\n        if row is not None:\n            return (row[1], int(row[0]))\n\n    return (None, None)\n\n\ndef _lookup_value_concept(\n    engine: Any, value_text: str\n) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Query concept where domain_id='Meas Value' for SNOMED qualifiers.\n\n    Searches for categorical value terms like \"positive\", \"negative\",\n    \"normal\", \"abnormal\" in the Meas Value domain.\n\n    Args:\n        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.\n        value_text: Raw value text (e.g. \"positive\", \"absent\").\n\n    Returns:\n        (normalized_text, omop_concept_id) or (None, None) if not found.\n    \"\"\"\n    key = value_text.strip()\n    if not key:\n        return (None, None)\n\n    sql = text(\n        \"SELECT concept_id, concept_name \"\n        \"FROM concept \"\n        \"WHERE domain_id = 'Meas Value' \"\n        \"  AND standard_concept = 'S' \"\n        \"  AND LOWER(concept_name) = LOWER(:value_text) \"\n        \"LIMIT 1\"\n    )\n    with engine.connect() as conn:\n        row = conn.execute(sql, {\"value_text\": key}).fetchone()\n        if row is not None:\n            return (row[1].lower(), int(row[0]))\n\n        # Try synonym match\n        sql_syn = text(\n            \"SELECT c.concept_id, c.concept_name \"\n            \"FROM concept_synonym s \"\n            \"JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id \"\n            \"WHERE c.domain_id = 'Meas Value' \"\n            \"  AND c.standard_concept = 'S' \"\n            \"  AND LOWER(s.concept_synonym_name) = LOWER(:value_text) \"\n            \"LIMIT 1\"\n        )\n        row = conn.execute(sql_syn, {\"value_text\": key}).fetchone()\n        if row is not None:\n            return (row[1].lower(), int(row[0]))\n\n    return (None, None)\n\n\ndef _lookup_ordinal_concept(\n    engine: Any, scale_name: str, grade: str\n) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Query concept + concept_synonym for a scale+grade combination.\n\n    Searches for ordinal scale values (e.g. \"ECOG 2\", \"NYHA class III\")\n    in the OMOP vocabulary.\n\n    Args:\n        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.\n        scale_name: Canonical scale name (e.g. \"ecog\", \"karnofsky\").\n        grade: Grade/level value (e.g. \"2\", \"80\").\n\n    Returns:\n        omop_concept_id or None if not found.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Build search patterns for the scale + grade combo\n    patterns = [\n        f\"{scale_name} {grade}\",\n        f\"{scale_name} grade {grade}\",\n        f\"{scale_name} performance status {grade}\",\n        f\"{scale_name} score {grade}\",\n        f\"{scale_name} class {grade}\",\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        sql = text(\n            \"SELECT c.concept_id \"\n            \"FROM concept c \"\n            \"WHERE c.standard_concept = 'S' \"\n            \"  AND LOWER(c.concept_name) LIKE LOWER(:pattern) \"\n            \"LIMIT 1\"\n        )\n        with engine.connect() as conn:\n            row = conn.execute(sql, {\"pattern\": f\"%{pattern}%\"}).fetchone()\n            if row is not None:\n                return int(row[0])\n\n        # Try synonyms\n        sql_syn = text(\n            \"SELECT c.concept_id \"\n            \"FROM concept_synonym s \"\n            \"JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id \"\n            \"WHERE c.standard_concept = 'S' \"\n            \"  AND LOWER(s.concept_synonym_name) LIKE LOWER(:pattern) \"\n            \"LIMIT 1\"\n        )\n        with engine.connect() as conn:\n            row = conn.execute(sql_syn, {\"pattern\": f\"%{pattern}%\"}).fetchone()\n            if row is not None:\n                return int(row[0])\n\n    return None\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Public async API\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nasync def lookup_omop_concept("
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/unit_normalizer.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Unit and value normalizer: UCUM lookup with OMOP concept ID resolution.\n\nQueries the OMOP vocabulary database for UCUM unit codes, categorical value\nconcepts, and ordinal scale grades. All lookups are cached via lru_cache\nfor performance.\n\nNo LLM calls — pure DB lookup with alias expansion and case-insensitive\nmatching.  Returns (None, None) for unrecognized inputs; never raises.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport re\nfrom functools import lru_cache\nfrom typing import Any\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"^(?:grade|stage|class|score|level)\\s*\",\n    re.IGNORECASE,\n)\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Clinical knowledge: ordinal scale alias -> canonical scale name\n# (This mapping is not in OMOP — it's clinical domain knowledge about\n# which entity names refer to which performance/grading scale.)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES: dict[str, str] = {\n    \"ecog\": \"ecog\",\n    \"ecog ps\": \"ecog\",\n    \"ecog performance status\": \"ecog\",\n    \"eastern cooperative oncology group\": \"ecog\",\n    \"who performance status\": \"ecog\",\n    \"zubrod score\": \"ecog\",\n    \"karnofsky\": \"karnofsky\",\n    \"kps\": \"karnofsky\",\n    \"karnofsky performance status\": \"karnofsky\",\n    \"karnofsky performance scale\": \"karnofsky\",\n    \"nyha\": \"nyha\",\n    \"nyha class\": \"nyha\",\n    \"nyha functional class\": \"nyha\",\n    \"nyha classification\": \"nyha\",\n    \"new york heart association\": \"nyha\",\n    \"asa\": \"asa\",\n    \"asa physical status\": \"asa\",\n    \"asa ps\": \"asa\",\n    \"asa class\": \"asa\",\n    \"asa classification\": \"asa\",\n    \"asa score\": \"asa\",\n    \"american society of anesthesiologists\": \"asa\",\n}\n\n# Unit concept ID for ordinal scores — always 8527 ({score})\n_ORDINAL_UNIT_CONCEPT_ID = 8527\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# DB-backed unit normalization\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@lru_cache(maxsize=512)\ndef _cached_ucum_lookup(unit_key: str) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Cached wrapper around _lookup_ucum_unit (keyed on lowercased unit text).\"\"\"\n    from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import _get_omop_engine, _lookup_ucum_unit\n\n    try:\n        engine = _get_omop_engine()\n    except RuntimeError:\n        logger.debug(\"OMOP_VOCAB_URL not set — unit lookup unavailable\")\n        return (None, None)\n    return _lookup_ucum_unit(engine, unit_key)\n\n\ndef normalize_unit(unit_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Normalize a unit string to its UCUM code and OMOP unit_concept_id.\n\n    Args:\n        unit_text: Raw unit text (e.g. \"mg/dL\", \"%\", \"years\").\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (ucum_code, omop_unit_concept_id), or (None, None)\n        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.\n    \"\"\"\n    if unit_text is not None and not isinstance(unit_text, str):\n        unit_text = str(unit_text)\n    if not unit_text or not unit_text.strip():\n        return (None, None)\n\n    key = unit_text.strip().lower()\n    return _cached_ucum_lookup(key)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# DB-backed categorical value normalization\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@lru_cache(maxsize=128)\ndef _cached_value_lookup(value_key: str) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Cached wrapper around _lookup_value_concept (keyed on lowercased value text).\"\"\"\n    from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import (\n        _get_omop_engine,\n        _lookup_value_concept,\n    )\n\n    try:\n        engine = _get_omop_engine()\n    except RuntimeError:\n        logger.debug(\"OMOP_VOCAB_URL not set — value lookup unavailable\")\n        return (None, None)\n    return _lookup_value_concept(engine, value_key)\n\n\ndef normalize_value(\n    value_text: str | None,\n) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Normalize a categorical value to its SNOMED code and OMOP concept ID.\n\n    Args:\n        value_text: Raw value text (e.g. \"positive\", \"negative\", \"normal\").\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (normalized_text, omop_value_concept_id), or (None, None)\n        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.\n    \"\"\"\n    if value_text is not None and not isinstance(value_text, str):\n        value_text = str(value_text)\n    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():\n        return (None, None)\n\n    key = value_text.strip().lower()\n    return _cached_value_lookup(key)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Ordinal scale normalization\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text: str) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Match entity text to an ordinal scale key.\n\n    Uses three strategies in order:\n    1. Exact alias match (case-insensitive)\n    2. Entity text is a substring of an alias\n    3. An alias is a substring of entity text\n\n    Returns:\n        Scale key (e.g. \"ecog\") or None if no match.\n    \"\"\"\n    key = entity_text.strip().lower()\n\n    # Strategy 1: exact alias match\n    if key in ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES:\n        return ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES[key]\n\n    # Strategy 2 & 3: bidirectional substring containment\n    for alias, scale_key in ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES.items():\n        if key in alias or alias in key:\n            return scale_key\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef normalize_ordinal_value(\n    value_text: str | None,\n    entity_text: str | None = None,\n) -> tuple[int | None, int | None] | None:\n    \"\"\"Entity-context-aware ordinal value normalization.\n\n    Returns:\n        (value_concept_id, unit_concept_id) if entity matches an ordinal scale.\n        value_concept_id is the OMOP ID from DB lookup (or None if not found).\n        unit_concept_id is always 8527 ({score}) for ordinal scales.\n        Returns None (not a tuple) if entity doesn't match any ordinal scale.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():\n        return None\n\n    scale_key = _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text)\n    if scale_key is None:\n        return None\n\n    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():\n        return (None, _ORDINAL_UNIT_CONCEPT_ID)\n\n    # Normalize value: strip ordinal prefixes, convert \"2.0\" -> \"2\"\n    cleaned = _ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE.sub(\"\", value_text.strip())\n    try:\n        numeric = float(cleaned)\n        if numeric == int(numeric):\n            cleaned = str(int(numeric))\n        else:\n            cleaned = str(numeric)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        cleaned = cleaned.strip()\n\n    # Look up the grade in the OMOP vocabulary\n    from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import (\n        _get_omop_engine,\n        _lookup_ordinal_concept,\n    )\n\n    try:\n        engine = _get_omop_engine()\n        omop_value_cid = _lookup_ordinal_concept(engine, scale_key, cleaned)\n    except RuntimeError:\n        logger.debug(\"OMOP_VOCAB_URL not set — ordinal lookup unavailable\")\n        omop_value_cid = None\n\n    return (omop_value_cid, _ORDINAL_UNIT_CONCEPT_ID)\n\n\ndef propose_ordinal_mappings() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Return an empty list — ordinal mappings are now resolved dynamically from the DB.\n\n    Retained for backward compatibility with callers that reference this function.\n    \"\"\"\n    return []\n"
}
```

```
     4→build_expression_tree and verifies that unit_concept_id=8527 ({score})

     6→are unaffected.
     7→
     8→Also tests the propose_ordinal_mappings() agent utility.

    33→@pytest.fixture()
    34→def engine():
    35→    eng = create_engine(
    36→        "sqlite:///:memory:",
    37→        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},

    39→    )
    40→    import shared.models  # noqa: F401

    49→@pytest.fixture()
    50→def session(engine) -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    51→    s = Session(engine)
    52→    try:

    54→    finally:
    55→        s.close()

    58→def _setup(session: Session) -> tuple[str, str]:
    59→    protocol = Protocol(title="NCT-ORDINAL-E2E", file_uri="local://test.pdf")
    60→    session.add(protocol)
    61→    session.flush()
    62→    batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
    63→    session.add(batch)
    64→    session.flush()
    65→    return protocol.id, batch.id
    66→
    67→
    68→def _make_crit(session: Session, batch_id: str, text: str) -> Criteria:
    69→    c = Criteria(batch_id=batch_id, criteria_type="inclusion", text=text)
    70→    session.add(c)
    71→    session.flush()
    72→    return c

   240→class TestPhase3bOrdinalE2E:
   241→    """E2E: ordinal criteria through build_expression_tree → DB."""
   242→
   243→    async def test_ordinal_criteria_get_score_unit(self, session) -> None:
   244→        """All ordinal criteria get unit_concept_id=8527 ({score})."""
   245→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup(session)
   246→
   247→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   248→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   249→
   250→            for i, crit_def in enumerate(ORDINAL_CRITERIA):
   251→                crit = _make_crit(session, batch_id, crit_def["text"])
   252→                await build_expression_tree(
   253→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   254→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   255→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   256→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   257→                    inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   258→                    session=session,
   259→                )
   260→                session.flush()
   261→
   262→                atomics = session.exec(
   263→                    select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   264→                        AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id
   265→                    )
   266→                ).all()
   267→                assert len(atomics) == 1, f"Criterion {i}: expected 1 atomic"
   268→
   269→                a = atomics[0]
   270→                exp = crit_def["expect"]
   271→
   272→                assert a.unit_concept_id == exp["unit_concept_id"], (
   273→                    f"Criterion {i} ({crit_def['text']}): "
   274→                    f"unit_concept_id={a.unit_concept_id}, "
   275→                    f"expected {exp['unit_concept_id']}"
   276→                )
   277→
   278→                if "value_numeric" in exp:
   279→                    assert a.value_numeric == pytest.approx(exp["value_numeric"]), (
   280→                        f"Criterion {i}: value_numeric"
   281→                    )
   282→
   283→                if "value_concept_id" in exp:
   284→                    assert a.value_concept_id == exp["value_concept_id"], (
   285→                        f"Criterion {i}: value_concept_id"
   286→                    )
   287→
   288→        session.commit()
   289→
   290→    async def test_ecog_grade_prefix_through_backend(self, session) -> None:
   291→        """'Grade 2' value text resolves through full backend path."""
   292→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup(session)
   293→
   294→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   295→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   296→
   297→            crit = _make_crit(session, batch_id, "ECOG Grade 2")
   298→            await build_expression_tree(
   299→                criterion_text="ECOG Grade 2",
   300→                field_mappings=[
   301→                    {
   302→                        "entity": "ECOG",
   303→                        "relation": "=",
   304→                        "value": "Grade 2",
   305→                        "unit": None,
   306→                    },
   307→                ],
   308→                criterion_id=crit.id,
   309→                protocol_id=protocol_id,
   310→                inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   311→                session=session,
   312→            )
   313→            session.flush()
   314→
   315→            atomics = session.exec(
   316→                select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id)
   317→            ).all()
   318→            assert len(atomics) == 1
   319→            # "Grade 2" parses to numeric because _parse_value can't parse it,
   320→            # so value_text="Grade 2" and value_numeric=None
   321→            # But the ordinal normalizer still matches the entity and sets
   322→            # unit_concept_id=8527
   323→            assert atomics[0].unit_concept_id == 8527
   324→

   357→    async def test_mixed_ordinal_and_lab_criteria(self, session) -> None:
   358→        """Mixed criteria: ordinal + lab values in same batch work correctly."""
   359→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup(session)
   360→
   361→        mixed = [
   362→            # Ordinal
   363→            {
   364→                "text": "ECOG <= 1",
   365→                "mappings": [
   366→                    {
   367→                        "entity": "ECOG",
   368→                        "relation": "<=",
   369→                        "value": "1",
   370→                        "unit": None,
   371→                    },
   372→                ],
   373→            },
   374→            # Lab value
   375→            {
   376→                "text": "Creatinine <= 1.5 mg/dL",
   377→                "mappings": [
   378→                    {
   379→                        "entity": "Serum creatinine",
   380→                        "relation": "<=",
   381→                        "value": "1.5",
   382→                        "unit": "mg/dL",
   383→                    },
   384→                ],
   385→            },
   386→            # Another ordinal
   387→            {
   388→                "text": "NYHA class <= 2",
   389→                "mappings": [
   390→                    {
   391→                        "entity": "NYHA class",
   392→                        "relation": "<=",
   393→                        "value": "2",
   394→                        "unit": None,
   395→                    },
   396→                ],
   397→            },
   398→        ]
   399→
   400→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   401→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   402→
   403→            crit_ids = []
   404→            for crit_def in mixed:
   405→                crit = _make_crit(session, batch_id, crit_def["text"])
   406→                crit_ids.append(crit.id)
   407→                await build_expression_tree(
   408→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   409→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   410→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   411→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   412→                    inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   413→                    session=session,
   414→                )
   415→                session.flush()
   416→
   417→        session.commit()
   418→
   419→        # ECOG: unit_concept_id=8527
   420→        ecog = session.exec(
   421→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit_ids[0])
   422→        ).first()
   423→        assert ecog is not None
   424→        assert ecog.unit_concept_id == 8527
   425→
   426→        # Creatinine: unit_concept_id=8840 (mg/dL)
   427→        creat = session.exec(
   428→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit_ids[1])
   429→        ).first()
   430→        assert creat is not None
   431→        assert creat.unit_concept_id == 8840
   432→
   433→        # NYHA: unit_concept_id=8527
   434→        nyha = session.exec(
   435→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit_ids[2])
   436→        ).first()
   437→        assert nyha is not None
   438→        assert nyha.unit_concept_id == 8527

   440→
   441→class TestProposeOrdinalMappingsE2E:
   442→    """Test the agent proposal utility end-to-end."""
   443→
   444→    def test_all_grades_need_resolution(self) -> None:
   445→        """Since no omop_value_concept_id is set, all grades need resolution."""
   446→        missing = propose_ordinal_mappings()
   447→        assert len(missing) > 0
   448→
   449→        # Should include entries from all 3 scales
   450→        scales = {e["scale"] for e in missing}
   451→        assert "ecog" in scales
   452→        assert "karnofsky" in scales
   453→        assert "nyha" in scales
   454→
   455→    def test_ecog_grades_in_proposals(self) -> None:
   456→        """All 6 ECOG grades appear in proposals."""
   457→        missing = propose_ordinal_mappings()
   458→        ecog_grades = {e["grade"] for e in missing if e["scale"] == "ecog"}
   459→        assert ecog_grades == {"0", "1", "2", "3", "4", "5"}
   460→
   461→    def test_proposals_have_snomed_codes(self) -> None:
   462→        """ECOG proposals include SNOMED codes from YAML."""
   463→        missing = propose_ordinal_mappings()
   464→        ecog_entries = [e for e in missing if e["scale"] == "ecog"]
   465→        snomed_codes = [e["snomed_code"] for e in ecog_entries if e["snomed_code"]]
   466→        # All 6 ECOG grades have SNOMED codes
   467→        assert len(snomed_codes) == 6
   468→
   469→    def test_karnofsky_proposals_no_snomed(self) -> None:
   470→        """Karnofsky proposals have no SNOMED codes (not in YAML)."""
   471→        missing = propose_ordinal_mappings()
   472→        kps_entries = [e for e in missing if e["scale"] == "karnofsky"]
   473→        assert all(e["snomed_code"] is None for e in kps_entries)
   474→
```

```
     1→"""E2E test configuration and fixtures.
     2→
     3→Provides Docker Compose stack detection, automatic skip when the stack is
     4→unavailable, and core fixtures (authenticated HTTP client, direct DB session,
     5→PDF upload factory, database cleanup) for end-to-end tests against the real
     6→running services.
     7→
     8→Usage:
     9→    Mark any E2E test with ``@pytest.mark.e2e``. When the Docker Compose stack
    10→    is not running, all e2e-marked tests are automatically skipped (exit 0).
    11→"""

    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import base64
```
