    36→
    37→_DEFAULT_API_URL = "http://localhost:8000"
    38→_DEFAULT_DATABASE_URL = "postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/app"
    39→
    40→
    41→def _check_stack() -> bool:
    42→    """Check whether the Docker Compose stack (API + PostgreSQL) is reachable."""
    43→    api_url = os.getenv("E2E_API_URL", _DEFAULT_API_URL)
    44→    try:
    45→        resp = httpx.get(f"{api_url}/health", timeout=3.0)
    46→        if resp.status_code != 200:
    47→            return False
    48→    except (httpx.ConnectError, httpx.TimeoutException, Exception):
    49→        return False
    50→
    51→    # Also verify PostgreSQL is reachable
    52→    try:
    53→        from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
    54→
    55→        db_url = os.getenv("E2E_DATABASE_URL", _DEFAULT_DATABASE_URL)
    56→        engine = create_engine(db_url, connect_args={"connect_timeout": 3})
    57→        with engine.connect() as conn:
    58→            conn.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
    59→        engine.dispose()
    60→    except Exception:
    61→        return False
    62→
    63→    return True
    64→
    65→
    66→def _wants_e2e(config: pytest.Config) -> bool:
    67→    """Return True only if the user explicitly requested e2e tests.
    68→
    69→    This guard prevents the health-check from adding latency to regular
    70→    ``uv run pytest`` invocations that do not target E2E tests.
    71→    """
    72→    # Check -m flag for e2e marker expression
    73→    markexpr: str = config.getoption("-m", default="")
    74→    if markexpr and "e2e" in markexpr:
    75→        return True
    76→
    77→    # Check if any explicit test path is under tests/e2e/
    78→    args: list[str] = config.getoption("file_or_dir", default=[]) or []
    79→    for arg in args:
    80→        if "tests/e2e" in str(arg) or "tests\\e2e" in str(arg):
    81→            return True
    82→
    83→    return False
    84→
    85→
    86→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    87→# Pytest hooks
    88→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89→
    90→
    91→def pytest_configure(config: pytest.Config) -> None:
    92→    """Detect Docker Compose availability once per session (if e2e requested)."""
    93→    global _STACK_AVAILABLE
    94→    if _wants_e2e(config):
    95→        _STACK_AVAILABLE = _check_stack()
    96→    else:
    97→        # Don't waste time checking -- no e2e tests requested
    98→        _STACK_AVAILABLE = False
    99→
   100→
   101→def pytest_collection_modifyitems(
   102→    config: pytest.Config, items: list[pytest.Item]
   103→) -> None:
   104→    """Auto-skip all ``@pytest.mark.e2e`` tests when the stack is unavailable."""
   105→    if _STACK_AVAILABLE:
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    skip_marker = pytest.mark.skip(
   109→        reason=(
   110→            "Docker Compose stack not running "
   111→            "(API at localhost:8000 or PostgreSQL at localhost:5432 unreachable)"
   112→        )
   113→    )
   114→    for item in items:
   115→        if "e2e" in item.keywords:
   116→            item.add_marker(skip_marker)
   117→
   118→
   119→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   120→# Session-scoped fixtures
   121→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   122→
   123→
   124→@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
   125→def e2e_api_url() -> str:
   126→    """Base URL for the running API service.
   127→
   128→    Reads from ``E2E_API_URL`` env var, defaulting to ``http://localhost:8000``.
   129→    """
   130→    return os.getenv("E2E_API_URL", _DEFAULT_API_URL)
   131→
   132→
   133→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   134→# Function-scoped fixtures
   135→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   136→
   137→
   138→@pytest.fixture()
   139→def e2e_db_session():
   140→    """Provide a SQLModel Session connected to the real Docker Compose PostgreSQL.
   141→
   142→    The session targets the database specified by ``E2E_DATABASE_URL`` (default:
   143→    ``postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/app``).
   144→
   145→    .. note::
   146→        This session does NOT auto-rollback. E2E tests operate on the real
   147→        database; cleanup is handled by dedicated cleanup fixtures in later
   148→        plans.
   149→    """
   150→    from sqlalchemy import create_engine
   151→    from sqlmodel import Session
   152→
   153→    db_url = os.getenv("E2E_DATABASE_URL", _DEFAULT_DATABASE_URL)
   154→    engine = create_engine(db_url)
   155→    with Session(engine) as session:
   156→        yield session
   157→    engine.dispose()
   158→
   159→
   160→@pytest.fixture()
   161→def e2e_api_client(e2e_api_url: str) -> Generator[httpx.Client, None, None]:
   162→    """Provide an authenticated ``httpx.Client`` pointed at the running API.
   163→
   164→    The client includes a ``Bearer`` JWT in the ``Authorization`` header,
   165→    signed with the same secret the API uses (``JWT_SECRET_KEY`` env var,
   166→    default: ``dev-secret-key-change-in-production``).
   167→
   168→    The test JWT contains claims for a synthetic E2E test user.
   169→    """
   170→    secret = os.getenv("JWT_SECRET_KEY", "dev-secret-key-change-in-production")
   171→    token = jwt.encode(
   172→        {"sub": "e2e-test-user", "email": "e2e@test.com", "name": "E2E Test"},
   173→        secret,
   174→        algorithm="HS256",
   175→    )
   176→    client = httpx.Client(
   177→        base_url=e2e_api_url,
   178→        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
   179→        timeout=30.0,
   180→    )
   181→    yield client
   182→    client.close()
   183→
   184→
   185→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   186→# Protocol tracking (for cleanup)
   187→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   188→
   189→
   190→@pytest.fixture()
   191→def _created_protocol_ids() -> list[str]:
   192→    """Accumulate protocol IDs created during a single test function."""
   193→    return []
   194→
   195→
   196→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→# Upload fixture (factory pattern)
   198→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   199→
   200→
   201→@pytest.fixture()
   202→def upload_test_pdf(
   203→    e2e_api_client: httpx.Client,
   204→    _created_protocol_ids: list[str],
   205→) -> Callable[..., str]:
   206→    """Factory fixture: upload a real PDF through the API upload flow.
   207→
   208→    Returns a callable ``upload_test_pdf(pdf_path=None) -> protocol_id``.
   209→    The upload follows the same three-step flow the frontend uses:
   210→
   211→    1. POST /protocols/upload  (get signed/local upload URL)
   212→    2. PUT pdf bytes to the upload URL
   213→    3. POST /protocols/{id}/confirm-upload
   214→
   215→    Requires ``USE_LOCAL_STORAGE=1`` on the API container so that the
   216→    upload URL points to the local-upload endpoint.
   217→    """
   218→
   219→    def _upload(pdf_path: str | None = None) -> str:
   220→        path = Path(pdf_path or _DEFAULT_TEST_PDF)
   221→        if not path.is_absolute():
   222→            # Resolve relative to repo root
   223→            repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
   224→            path = repo_root / path
   225→        assert path.exists(), f"Test PDF not found: {path}"
   226→
   227→        pdf_bytes = path.read_bytes()
   228→
   229→        # Step 1: Request upload URL
   230→        resp = e2e_api_client.post(
   231→            "/protocols/upload",
   232→            json={
   233→                "filename": "test-e2e.pdf",
   234→                "content_type": "application/pdf",
   235→                "file_size_bytes": len(pdf_bytes),
   236→            },
   237→        )
   238→        assert resp.status_code == 200, (
   239→            f"Upload request failed ({resp.status_code}): {resp.text}"
   240→        )
   241→        data = resp.json()
   242→        protocol_id = data["protocol_id"]
   243→        upload_url = data["upload_url"]
   244→
   245→        # Step 2: PUT the PDF bytes to the upload URL
   246→        put_resp = httpx.put(
   247→            upload_url,
   248→            content=pdf_bytes,
   249→            headers={"Content-Type": "application/pdf"},
   250→            timeout=30.0,
   251→        )
   252→        assert put_resp.status_code == 200, (
   253→            f"PUT to upload URL failed ({put_resp.status_code}): {put_resp.text}"
   254→        )
   255→
   256→        # Step 3: Confirm upload (with base64 PDF for quality scoring)
   257→        pdf_b64 = base64.b64encode(pdf_bytes).decode("ascii")
   258→        confirm_resp = e2e_api_client.post(
   259→            f"/protocols/{protocol_id}/confirm-upload",
   260→            json={"pdf_bytes_base64": pdf_b64},
   261→        )
   262→        assert confirm_resp.status_code == 200, (
   263→            f"Confirm upload failed ({confirm_resp.status_code}): {confirm_resp.text}"
   264→        )
   265→
   266→        _created_protocol_ids.append(protocol_id)
   267→        logger.info("Uploaded test PDF -> protocol_id=%s", protocol_id)
   268→        return protocol_id
   269→
   270→    return _upload
   271→
   272→
   273→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   274→# Database cleanup (autouse for all tests in tests/e2e/)
   275→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   276→
   277→
   278→@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
   279→def e2e_cleanup(
   280→    e2e_db_session,  # noqa: ANN001
   281→    _created_protocol_ids: list[str],
   282→):
   283→    """Delete all test-created protocols and related data after each test.
   284→
   285→    Deletion order respects foreign key constraints:
   286→    entities -> reviews -> audit_log -> criteria -> criteria_batches ->
   287→    outbox_events -> protocol
   288→    """
   289→    yield  # Run the test
   290→
   291→    if not _created_protocol_ids:
   292→        return
   293→
   294→    from sqlmodel import col, delete, select
   295→
   296→    from shared.models import (
   297→        AuditLog,
   298→        Criteria,
   299→        CriteriaBatch,
   300→        Entity,
   301→        OutboxEvent,
   302→        Protocol,
   303→        Review,
   304→    )
   305→
   306→    session = e2e_db_session
   307→
   308→    for protocol_id in _created_protocol_ids:
   309→        # Gather batch IDs for this protocol
   310→        batch_ids: list[str] = [
   311→            row
   312→            for row in session.exec(
   313→                select(CriteriaBatch.id).where(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id == protocol_id)
   314→            ).all()
   315→        ]
   316→
   317→        # Gather criteria IDs across all batches
   318→        criteria_ids: list[str] = []
   319→        if batch_ids:
   320→            criteria_ids = [
   321→                row
   322→                for row in session.exec(
   323→                    select(Criteria.id).where(col(Criteria.batch_id).in_(batch_ids))
   324→                ).all()
   325→            ]
   326→
   327→        # 1. Delete entities for these criteria
   328→        if criteria_ids:
   329→            session.exec(
   330→                delete(Entity).where(col(Entity.criteria_id).in_(criteria_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   331→            )
   332→
   333→        # 2. Delete reviews for these criteria
   334→        if criteria_ids:
   335→            session.exec(
   336→                delete(Review).where(col(Review.target_id).in_(criteria_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   337→            )
   338→
   339→        # 3. Delete audit log entries related to this protocol or its criteria
   340→        target_ids = [protocol_id, *criteria_ids]
   341→        session.exec(
   342→            delete(AuditLog).where(col(AuditLog.target_id).in_(target_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   343→        )
   344→
   345→        # 4. Delete criteria
   346→        if batch_ids:
   347→            session.exec(
   348→                delete(Criteria).where(col(Criteria.batch_id).in_(batch_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   349→            )
   350→
   351→        # 5. Delete criteria batches
   352→        if batch_ids:
   353→            session.exec(
   354→                delete(CriteriaBatch).where(col(CriteriaBatch.id).in_(batch_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   355→            )
   356→
   357→        # 6. Delete outbox events for this protocol
   358→        session.exec(
   359→            delete(OutboxEvent).where(
   360→                OutboxEvent.aggregate_id == protocol_id  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   361→            )
   362→        )
   363→
   364→        # 7. Delete the protocol itself
   365→        session.exec(
   366→            delete(Protocol).where(Protocol.id == protocol_id)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   367→        )
   368→
   369→    session.commit()
   370→    logger.info(
   371→        "E2E cleanup: deleted %d protocol(s) and related data",
   372→        len(_created_protocol_ids),
   373→    )
   374→
   375→
   376→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   377→# Pipeline wait utility
   378→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   379→
   380→_TERMINAL_STATUSES = frozenset(
   381→    {
   382→        "pending_review",
   383→        "complete",
   384→        "extraction_failed",
   385→        "grounding_failed",
   386→        "pipeline_failed",
   387→        "dead_letter",
   388→    }
   389→)
   390→
   391→
   392→def wait_for_pipeline(
   393→    api_client: httpx.Client,
   394→    protocol_id: str,
   395→    timeout: int = 180,
   396→    poll_interval: int = 5,
   397→) -> dict:
   398→    """Poll protocol status until it reaches a terminal state.
   399→
   400→    Args:
   401→        api_client: Authenticated httpx client.
   402→        protocol_id: Protocol to monitor.
   403→        timeout: Maximum seconds to wait.
   404→        poll_interval: Seconds between polls.
   405→
   406→    Returns:
   407→        The final protocol response dict.
   408→
   409→    Raises:
   410→        TimeoutError: If timeout is exceeded before reaching terminal state.
   411→    """
   412→    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
   413→    while True:
   414→        resp = api_client.get(f"/protocols/{protocol_id}")
   415→        resp.raise_for_status()
   416→        data = resp.json()
   417→
   418→        status = data.get("status", "")
   419→        if status in _TERMINAL_STATUSES:
   420→            logger.info(
   421→                "Pipeline reached terminal status '%s' for %s",
   422→                status,
   423→                protocol_id,
   424→            )
   425→            return data
   426→
   427→        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
   428→            raise TimeoutError(
   429→                f"Protocol {protocol_id} still in '{status}' after {timeout}s"
   430→            )
   431→
   432→        time.sleep(poll_interval)
   433→

     1→"""Full-cycle E2E test: Lookup → Agent → Approve → Persist.
     2→
     3→Demonstrates the complete ordinal resolution lifecycle:
     4→
     5→Run 1 (unknown scale):
     6→  1. Child-Pugh criterion through build_expression_tree
     7→     → normalize_ordinal_value() returns None (not in YAML)
     8→     → AtomicCriterion.unit_concept_id = None
     9→  2. ordinal_resolve_node fires → LLM (mocked) confirms ordinal
    10→     → unit_concept_id updated to 8527
    11→     → AuditLog proposal written for human review
    12→
    13→Simulated approval:
    14→  3. Add Child-Pugh to ordinal_scales config (simulates human approval)
    15→
    16→Run 2 (known scale):
    17→  4. New Child-Pugh criterion through build_expression_tree
    18→     → normalize_ordinal_value() NOW matches (in YAML)
    19→     → AtomicCriterion.unit_concept_id = 8527 at creation time
    20→  5. ordinal_resolve_node finds no candidates → no LLM call
    21→"""
    22→
    23→from __future__ import annotations
    24→
    25→import json
    26→import os
    27→from typing import Any, Generator
    28→from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, patch
    29→
    30→import pytest
    31→from shared.models import (
    32→    AtomicCriterion,
    33→    AuditLog,
    34→    Criteria,
    35→    CriteriaBatch,
    36→    Protocol,
    37→)
    38→from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool
    39→from sqlmodel import Session, SQLModel, create_engine, select
    40→
    41→from protocol_processor.schemas.ordinal import (
    42→    OrdinalResolutionResponse,
    43→    OrdinalScaleProposal,
    44→)
    45→from protocol_processor.tools.structure_builder import (
    46→    build_expression_tree,
    47→)
    48→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import (
    49→    _load_ordinal_scales,
    50→    normalize_ordinal_value,
    51→)
    52→
    53→# ── Fixtures ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    54→
    55→
    56→@pytest.fixture()
    57→def engine():
    58→    eng = create_engine(
    59→        "sqlite:///:memory:",
    60→        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    61→        poolclass=StaticPool,
    62→    )
    63→    SQLModel.metadata.create_all(eng)
    64→    try:
    65→        yield eng
    66→    finally:
    67→        eng.dispose()
    68→
    69→
    70→@pytest.fixture()
    71→def session(engine) -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    72→    s = Session(engine)
    73→    try:
    74→        yield s
    75→    finally:
    76→        s.close()
    77→
    78→
    79→# ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    80→
    81→
    82→def _setup(session: Session) -> tuple[str, str]:
    83→    protocol = Protocol(
    84→        title="NCT-FULL-CYCLE",
    85→        file_uri="local://test.pdf",
    86→    )
    87→    session.add(protocol)
    88→    session.flush()
    89→    batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
    90→    session.add(batch)
    91→    session.flush()
    92→    return protocol.id, batch.id
    93→
    94→
    95→def _make_crit(
    96→    session: Session,
    97→    batch_id: str,
    98→    text: str,
    99→) -> Criteria:
   100→    c = Criteria(
   101→        batch_id=batch_id,
   102→        criteria_type="inclusion",
   103→        text=text,
   104→    )
   105→    session.add(c)
   106→    session.flush()
   107→    return c
   108→
   109→
   110→CHILD_PUGH_APPROVED_CONFIG: dict[str, Any] = {
   111→    "entity_aliases": [
   112→        "Child-Pugh",
   113→        "Child-Pugh score",
   114→        "Child-Pugh classification",
   115→        "CTP score",
   116→    ],
   117→    "loinc_code": "75622-1",
   118→    "unit_concept_id": 8527,
   119→    "values": {
   120→        "5": {"description": "Class A (best)"},
   121→        "6": {"description": "Class A"},
   122→        "7": {"description": "Class B"},
   123→        "8": {"description": "Class B"},
   124→        "9": {"description": "Class B"},
   125→        "10": {"description": "Class C"},
   126→        "11": {"description": "Class C"},
   127→        "12": {"description": "Class C"},
   128→        "13": {"description": "Class C"},
   129→        "14": {"description": "Class C"},
   130→        "15": {"description": "Class C (worst)"},
   131→    },
   132→}
   133→
   134→
   135→# ── Full Cycle Test ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
   136→
   137→
   138→class TestOrdinalFullCycle:
   139→    """Full Lookup → Agent → Approve → Persist cycle."""
   140→
   141→    async def test_full_cycle_child_pugh(
   142→        self,
   143→        engine,
   144→        session,
   145→    ) -> None:
   146→        """Child-Pugh: unknown → LLM resolve → approve → static lookup."""
   147→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup(session)
   148→
   149→        # ── Phase 1: Static Lookup (MISS) ─────────────────────────
   150→        # Child-Pugh is NOT in the YAML config
   151→        assert normalize_ordinal_value("6", "Child-Pugh score") is None
   152→
   153→        # Build expression tree — creates AtomicCriterion with
   154→        # unit_concept_id=None (no YAML match, no physical unit)
   155→        crit1 = _make_crit(
   156→            session,
   157→            batch_id,
   158→            "Child-Pugh score <= 6",
   159→        )
   160→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   161→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   162→            await build_expression_tree(
   163→                criterion_text="Child-Pugh score <= 6",
   164→                field_mappings=[
   165→                    {
   166→                        "entity": "Child-Pugh score",
   167→                        "relation": "<=",
   168→                        "value": "6",
   169→                        "unit": None,
   170→                    }
   171→                ],
   172→                criterion_id=crit1.id,
   173→                protocol_id=protocol_id,
   174→                inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   175→                session=session,
   176→            )
   177→        session.flush()
   178→
   179→        atomic1 = session.exec(
   180→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   181→                AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit1.id,
   182→            )
   183→        ).first()
   184→        assert atomic1 is not None
   185→        assert atomic1.unit_concept_id is None, (
   186→            "Before LLM resolve: unit_concept_id should be None"
   187→        )
   188→        assert atomic1.value_numeric == pytest.approx(6.0)
   189→        session.commit()
   190→
   191→        # ── Phase 2: Agent Resolve (LLM) ──────────────────────────
   192→        # ordinal_resolve_node queries AtomicCriterion where
   193→        # unit_concept_id IS NULL + value_numeric IS NOT NULL +
   194→        # unit_text IS NULL → finds our Child-Pugh record
   195→        mock_response = OrdinalResolutionResponse(
   196→            proposals=[
   197→                OrdinalScaleProposal(
   198→                    entity_text="Child-Pugh score",
   199→                    is_ordinal_scale=True,
   200→                    confidence=0.95,
   201→                    scale_name="child_pugh",
   202→                    entity_aliases=[
   203→                        "Child-Pugh",
   204→                        "Child-Pugh classification",
   205→                    ],
   206→                    loinc_code="75622-1",
   207→                ),
   208→            ],
   209→        )
   210→
   211→        from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import (
   212→            ordinal_resolve_node,
   213→        )
   214→
   215→        state: dict[str, Any] = {
   216→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   217→            "batch_id": batch_id,
   218→            "error": None,
   219→            "errors": [],
   220→        }
   221→
   222→        with (
   223→            patch(
   224→                "protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve.engine",
   225→                engine,
   226→            ),
   227→            patch(
   228→                "langchain_google_genai.ChatGoogleGenerativeAI",
   229→            ) as mock_cls,
   230→            patch.dict(
   231→                os.environ,
   232→                {"GOOGLE_API_KEY": "test-key"},
   233→            ),
   234→        ):
   235→            mock_model = mock_cls.return_value
   236→            mock_structured = mock_model.with_structured_output.return_value
   237→            mock_structured.ainvoke = AsyncMock(return_value=mock_response)
   238→
   239→            result = await ordinal_resolve_node(
   240→                state,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   241→            )
   242→
   243→        assert result["status"] == "completed"
   244→
   245→        # Verify: unit_concept_id updated to 8527
   246→        session.expire_all()
   247→        atomic1_updated = session.get(AtomicCriterion, atomic1.id)
   248→        assert atomic1_updated is not None
   249→        assert atomic1_updated.unit_concept_id == 8527, (
   250→            "After LLM resolve: unit_concept_id should be 8527"
   251→        )
   252→
   253→        # Verify: AuditLog proposal written
   254→        audits = session.exec(
   255→            select(AuditLog).where(
   256→                AuditLog.event_type == "ordinal_scale_proposed",
   257→            )
   258→        ).all()
   259→        assert len(audits) == 1
   260→        assert audits[0].details["proposals"][0]["scale_name"] == ("child_pugh")
   261→
   262→        # Verify: proposals in state
   263→        assert result.get("ordinal_proposals_json") is not None
   264→        proposals = json.loads(result["ordinal_proposals_json"])
   265→        assert proposals[0]["entity_text"] == "Child-Pugh score"
   266→
   267→        # ── Phase 3: Simulate Approval ────────────────────────────
   268→        # Human reviews the AuditLog proposal and approves it.
   269→        # This adds Child-Pugh to the ordinal_scales YAML config.
   270→        # We simulate this by patching _load_ordinal_scales.
   271→        _load_ordinal_scales.cache_clear()

Perfect! Now I have comprehensive understanding. Let me compile my findings into a clear research report.

## GROUNDING PIPELINE RESEARCH SUMMARY

Based on my thorough examination of the codebase, here's how to trigger grounding and understand the pipeline flow:

### 1. FASTEST PATH TO SUBMIT TEXT & GET GROUNDING RESULTS

**Quick Start: Upload Protocol → Pipeline Auto-Processes → Get Results**

```
1. POST /protocols/upload
   - Returns: protocol_id + signed_url for PDF upload

2. Upload PDF to GCS via signed_url
   
3. POST /protocols/{protocol_id}/confirm-upload
   - Publishes ProtocolUploaded event via outbox
   - Triggers full pipeline (ingest → extract → parse → ground → persist)

4. Poll GET /protocols/{protocol_id} until status == "pending_review"
   - Or use GET /protocols/{protocol_id}/batches to see real-time batch creation

5. GET /reviews/batches/{batch_id}/criteria
   - Returns criteria with grounded entities including:
     - entity.grounding_confidence (0.0-1.0)
     - entity.umls_cui (UMLS concept unique identifier)
     - entity.snomed_code (SNOMED terminology)
     - entity.field_mappings (structured operator/value/unit)
```

**Files:**
- Endpoint: `/services/api-service/src/api_service/protocols.py` (upload, confirm-upload)
- Handler: `/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/trigger.py` (handle_protocol_uploaded)

---

### 2. API ENDPOINTS FOR GROUNDING WORKFLOW

**In `services/api-service/src/api_service/main.py`:**

Key routers included:
- `protocols_router` → `/protocols/upload`, `/protocols/{id}/confirm-upload`, `/protocols/{id}/retry`
- `reviews_router` → `/reviews/batches/{batch_id}/criteria` (fetch grounded results)
- `criterion_rerun_router` → `/reviews/criteria/{criterion_id}/rerun` (re-ground single criterion with feedback)

**Critical Endpoints:**

| Endpoint | Method | Purpose |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `/protocols/upload` | POST | Generate signed URL, create Protocol record |
| `/protocols/{id}/confirm-upload` | POST | Trigger pipeline via outbox event |
| `/protocols/{id}` | GET | Check protocol status (pending/processing/pending_review/grounding_failed) |
| `/protocols/{id}/batches` | GET | List batches (for protocol timeline) |
| `/reviews/batches/{batch_id}/criteria` | GET | Fetch criteria with entities + grounding results |
| `/protocols/{id}/retry` | POST | Resume failed protocol from LangGraph checkpoint |
| `/reviews/criteria/{id}/rerun` | POST | Re-ground single criterion with reviewer feedback |

---

### 3. GROUNDING PIPELINE ARCHITECTURE

**Pipeline Nodes (LangGraph 7-node workflow):**

```
START → ingest → extract → parse → ground → persist → structure → ordinal_resolve → END
         ↓        ↓        ↓                     ↓
      [Read PDF] [LLM]   [Parse  [Route        [Persist
                         Extract]  Entities      to DB]
                                  + MedGemma
                                  Select]
```

**Error Handling Strategy:**
- `ingest`, `extract`, `parse`: Fatal errors → route to END
- `ground`: Error accumulation → partial results preserved alongside successes
- `persist`, `structure`, `ordinal_resolve`: Error accumulation pattern

---

### 4. GROUNDING NODE DETAILS

**File:** `/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`

**Flow:**

```python
async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict:
    # 1. Parse entities_json (list of entities to ground)
    entity_items = json.loads(state["entities_json"])
    
    # 2. FOR EACH ENTITY (parallel, semaphore=4):
    #    a. Route through TerminologyRouter (UMLS/SNOMED APIs)
    #       → returns list of GroundingCandidate objects
    #    b. Call MedGemma (LLM) to select best match
    #       → returns EntityGroundingResult with selected_code, confidence
    #    c. Agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts):
    #       - If confidence < 0.5, ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions
    #       - Reformulate query, retry against terminology APIs
    #       - Mark as expert_review if all 3 attempts fail
    #    d. Parallel OMOP lookup (same entity)
    #    e. Reconcile TerminologyRouter + OMOP results
    #    f. Generate field_mappings (Entity, Operator, Value, Unit)
    #    g. Ground categorical values in field_mappings
    
    # 3. Batch-write all audit logs to DB
    
    # 4. Return grounded_entities_json (JSON array of EntityGroundingResult)
```

**Key Classes:**

- **GroundingCandidate** (schemas/grounding.py):
  ```python
  {
    "source_api": "umls_api",
    "code": "C0011847",
    "preferred_term": "Diabetes Mellitus",
    "semantic_type": "Disease or Syndrome",
    "score": 0.95
  }
  ```

- **EntityGroundingResult** (schemas/grounding.py):
  ```python
  {
    "entity_text": "Type 2 Diabetes",
    "entity_type": "Condition",
    "selected_code": "C0011847",
    "selected_system": "UMLS",
    "preferred_term": "Diabetes Mellitus",
    "confidence": 0.92,
    "candidates": [...],
    "reasoning": "...",
    "field_mappings": [...],
    "omop_concept_id": "201820",
    "reconciliation_status": "agree"
  }
  ```

---

### 5. HOW ENTITIES FLOW INTO GROUNDING

**File:** `/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/parse.py`

**Parse Node Process:**

```
1. Extract JSON → ExtractionResult model
   {criteria: [
     {text: "Serum creatinine >1.5 times ULN", criteria_type: "exclusion"}
   ]}

2. Create CriteriaBatch + Criteria DB records

3. FOR EACH CRITERION:
   a. Decompose into discrete medical entities
      (via entity_decomposer LLM call)
   b. Extract: entity_text, entity_type (Medication/Condition/Lab Value/etc)
   c. Build entity_items list with criterion_id threaded through

4. RETURN entities_json = JSON([{
     "text": "Serum creatinine",
     "entity_type": "Lab Value",
     "criterion_id": "uuid-1",
     "criterion_text": "Serum creatinine >1.5 times ULN"
   }])
```

**Entity Types Recognized:**
- Condition, Medication, Lab Value, Procedure, Genetic Factor, Demographics, Consent, Temporal, Assertion Status, etc.

---

### 6. STATE FLOW THROUGH PIPELINE

**File:** `/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/state.py`

```python
class PipelineState(TypedDict):
    # Input
    protocol_id: str          # UUID
    file_uri: str             # gs://bucket/path or local://path
    title: str                # Protocol title
    
    # Artifacts
    pdf_bytes: bytes | None   # Populated by ingest, cleared by parse
    extraction_json: str      # Populated by extract node
    entities_json: str        # Populated by parse (input to ground)
    grounded_entities_json: str  # OUTPUT from ground node ← KEY RESULT
    
    # Status
    status: Literal["processing", "completed", "failed"]
    error: str | None         # Fatal errors
    errors: list[str]         # Accumulated partial failures
```

---

### 8. TERMINOLOGY ROUTING STRATEGY

**File:** `/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/terminology_router.py`

The TerminologyRouter is entity-type-aware:

- **Condition**: Route to UMLS APIs (concept_linking, MetaMap)
- **Medication**: Route to RxNorm + UMLS
- **Lab Value**: Route to LOINC + UMLS
- **Procedure**: Route to SNOMED CT + UMLS
- **Genetic**: Route to genetic databases
- **Demographics** (age/gender): Agentic reasoning via MedGemma
- **Consent**: Skip (not groundable to medical codes)

Each entity type has configured APIs in YAML config, and the router queries them in parallel.

---

### 9. DUAL GROUNDING RECONCILIATION

**File:** `/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py` (lines 67-115)

Grounding occurs via two parallel paths:

1. **TerminologyRouter Path**: Entity text → UMLS/SNOMED APIs → GroundingCandidate list → MedGemma selection
2. **OMOP Path**: Entity text → OMOP Athena vocabulary → Direct concept lookup

Results are reconciled with fuzzy matching (token_set_ratio >= 80%):
- **"agree"**: Both paths found same code (high confidence)
- **"disagreement"**: Both found codes but different
- **"omop_missing"**: Only TerminologyRouter succeeded
- **"tooluniverse_missing"**: Only OMOP succeeded

---

    59→def _get_router() -> TerminologyRouter:

    67→def _reconcile_dual_grounding(
    68→    result: EntityGroundingResult,
    69→    omop_result: OmopLookupResult,
    70→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    71→    """Reconcile TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper results.
    72→
    73→    Sets omop_concept_id and reconciliation_status on the result:
    74→    - Both succeed → "agree" (high confidence) or "disagreement"
    75→    - Only TerminologyRouter → "omop_missing"
    76→    - Only OMOP → "tooluniverse_missing"
    77→    - Neither → no change (existing expert_review behavior)
    78→
    79→    Agreement is checked by comparing the OMOP concept_name against
    80→    the ToolUniverse preferred_term (fuzzy match).
    81→
    82→    Args:
    83→        result: EntityGroundingResult from TerminologyRouter path.
    84→        omop_result: OmopLookupResult from OMOP mapper path.
    85→
    86→    Returns:
    87→        The same EntityGroundingResult, mutated with OMOP fields.
    88→    """
    89→    tu_ok = result.selected_code is not None
    90→    omop_ok = omop_result.omop_concept_id is not None
    91→
    92→    if tu_ok and omop_ok:
    93→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
    94→        # Fuzzy agreement check using token_set_ratio (order-insensitive)
    95→        tu_term = (result.preferred_term or "").lower()
    96→        omop_term = (omop_result.omop_concept_name or "").lower()
    97→        if tu_term and omop_term and fuzz.token_set_ratio(tu_term, omop_term) >= 80:
    98→            result.reconciliation_status = "agree"
    99→        else:
   100→            result.reconciliation_status = "disagreement"
   101→        logger.info(
   102→            "Dual grounding for '%s': TU=%s, OMOP=%s, status=%s",
   103→            result.entity_text[:50],
   104→            result.selected_code,
   105→            omop_result.omop_concept_id,
   106→            result.reconciliation_status,
   107→        )
   108→    elif tu_ok and not omop_ok:
   109→        result.reconciliation_status = "omop_missing"
   110→    elif not tu_ok and omop_ok:
   111→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
   112→        result.reconciliation_status = "tooluniverse_missing"
   113→    # else: neither — leave as-is (expert_review path)
   114→
   115→    return result

   118→def _log_grounding_audit(
   119→    session: Session,
   120→    protocol_id: str,
   121→    criterion_id: str,
   122→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   123→    candidates: list[Any],
   124→    result: Any,
   125→) -> None:
   126→    """Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.
   127→
   128→    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,
   129→    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:
   130→    "All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged."
   131→
   132→    Args:
   133→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   134→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   135→        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.
   136→        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.
   137→        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   138→        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.
   139→    """
   140→    audit = AuditLog(
   141→        event_type="entity_grounded",
   142→        target_type="criteria",
   143→        target_id=criterion_id,
   144→        details={
   145→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   146→            "entity_text": result.entity_text,
   147→            "entity_type": result.entity_type,
   148→            "candidate_count": len(candidates),
   149→            "candidates": [
   150→                {
   151→                    "source_api": c.source_api,
   152→                    "code": c.code,
   153→                    "preferred_term": c.preferred_term,
   154→                    "score": c.score,
   155→                }
   156→                for c in candidates
   157→            ],
   158→            "selected_code": result.selected_code,
   159→            "selected_system": result.selected_system,
   160→            "preferred_term": result.preferred_term,
   161→            "confidence": result.confidence,
   162→            "reasoning": result.reasoning,
   163→            "omop_concept_id": result.omop_concept_id,
   164→            "reconciliation_status": result.reconciliation_status,
   165→        },
   166→    )
   167→    session.add(audit)
   168→
   169→
   170→def _flush_audit_logs(
   171→    protocol_id: str,
   172→    pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]],
   173→) -> None:
   174→    """Batch-write audit log entries in a single session.
   175→
   176→    Args:
   177→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   178→        pending_audits: List of (criterion_id, entity, candidates, result) tuples.
   179→    """
   180→    if not pending_audits:
   181→        return
   182→    try:
   183→        with Session(engine) as session:
   184→            for criterion_id, entity, candidates, result in pending_audits:
   185→                _log_grounding_audit(
   186→                    session,
   187→                    protocol_id,
   188→                    criterion_id,
   189→                    entity,
   190→                    candidates,
   191→                    result,
   192→                )
   193→            session.commit()
   194→    except Exception as audit_error:
   195→        logger.warning(
   196→            "Failed to batch-write %d AuditLog entries: %s",
   197→            len(pending_audits),
   198→            audit_error,
   199→        )
   200→
   201→
   202→async def _ground_entity_with_retry(
   203→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   204→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   205→    criterion_text: str,
   206→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult, int]:
   207→    """Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).
   208→
   209→    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:
   210→    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma
   211→    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
   212→    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)
   213→    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review
   214→
   215→    Args:
   216→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   217→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   218→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   219→
   220→    Returns:
   221→        Tuple of (EntityGroundingResult, attempt_count). May have expert_review
   222→        marker in reasoning.
   223→    """
   224→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   225→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   226→
   227→    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates
   228→    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
   229→
   230→    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty
   231→    apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   232→    if not candidates:
   233→        if not apis:
   234→            logger.info(
   235→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter"
   236→                " — no APIs configured for this type",
   237→                entity_text[:50],
   238→                entity_type,
   239→            )
   240→            # No APIs configured → route directly to expert_review
   241→            return (
   242→                EntityGroundingResult(
   243→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   244→                    entity_type=entity_type,
   245→                    selected_code=None,
   246→                    selected_system=None,
   247→                    preferred_term=None,
   248→                    confidence=0.0,
   249→                    candidates=[],
   250→                    reasoning=(
   251→                        "[expert_review] No terminology APIs configured "
   252→                        f"for entity type '{entity_type}'. "
   253→                        "Routed to expert review."
   254→                    ),
   255→                ),
   256→                1,
   257→            )
   258→        else:
   259→            logger.info(
   260→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs",
   261→                entity_text[:50],
   262→                entity_type,
   263→            )
   264→
   265→    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates
   266→    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
   267→
   268→    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total
   269→    attempt = 1
   270→    current_query = entity_text
   271→    while (
   272→        result.selected_code is None
   273→        and result.confidence < 0.5
   274→        and attempt < 3
   275→        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   276→    ):
   277→        attempt += 1
   278→        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(
   279→            entity={**entity, "_previous_query": current_query},
   280→            criterion_context=criterion_text,
   281→            router=router,
   282→            attempt=attempt,
   283→        )
   284→
   285→        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
   286→            logger.info(
   287→                "Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning"
   288→                " (attempt %d) — skipping",
   289→                entity_text[:50],
   290→                attempt,
   291→            )
   292→            return (
   293→                EntityGroundingResult(
   294→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   295→                    entity_type=entity_type,
   296→                    selected_code=None,
   297→                    selected_system=None,
   298→                    preferred_term=None,
   299→                    confidence=0.0,
   300→                    candidates=[],
   301→                    reasoning=(
   302→                        f"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): "
   303→                        f"{reasoning_result.reasoning}"
   304→                    ),
   305→                ),
   306→                attempt,
   307→            )
   308→
   309→        new_query = (
   310→            reasoning_result.rephrased_query
   311→            or reasoning_result.derived_term
   312→            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion
   313→            or current_query
   314→        )
   315→        current_query = new_query
   316→
   317→        logger.info(
   318→            "Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'",
   319→            attempt,
   320→            entity_text[:50],
   321→            new_query[:50],
   322→        )
   323→
   324→        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
   325→        if new_candidates:
   326→            retry_entity = {**entity, "text": new_query}
   327→            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)
   328→            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
   329→                # Success — preserve original entity_text
   330→                return (
   331→                    EntityGroundingResult(
   332→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   333→                        entity_type=result.entity_type,
   334→                        selected_code=result.selected_code,
   335→                        selected_system=result.selected_system,
   336→                        preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   337→                        confidence=result.confidence,
   338→                        candidates=result.candidates,
   339→                        reasoning=(
   340→                            f"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] "
   341→                            f"{result.reasoning}"
   342→                        ),
   343→                        field_mappings=result.field_mappings,
   344→                    ),
   345→                    attempt,
   346→                )
   347→
   348→    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success
   349→    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
   350→        logger.warning(
   351→            "Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after"
   352→            " %d failed grounding attempts",
   353→            entity_text[:50],
   354→            entity_type,
   355→            attempt,
   356→        )
   357→        return (
   358→            EntityGroundingResult(
   359→                entity_text=result.entity_text,
   360→                entity_type=result.entity_type,
   361→                selected_code=result.selected_code,
   362→                selected_system=result.selected_system,
   363→                preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   364→                confidence=result.confidence,
   365→                candidates=result.candidates,
   366→                reasoning=(
   367→                    f"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after "
   368→                    f"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. "
   369→                    f"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}"
   370→                ),
   371→            ),
   372→            attempt,
   373→        )
   374→
   375→    return (result, attempt)
   376→
   377→
   378→def _ground_categorical_values(
   379→    mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   380→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   381→    """Ground categorical values in field mappings using unit_normalizer.
   382→
   383→    For standard-type mappings with non-numeric values (e.g. "Severe",
   384→    "positive", "NYHA Class III"), attempt to resolve a value_concept_id
   385→    using the unit_normalizer's value lookup.
   386→
   387→    This is a best-effort post-processing step — failures don't block
   388→    the grounding result.
   389→
   390→    Args:
   391→        mappings: List of field mapping dicts from generate_field_mappings.
   392→
   393→    Returns:
   394→        The same list with value_concept_id populated where possible.
   395→    """
   396→    numeric_re = re.compile(r"^[\d.,\-]+$")
   397→
   398→    for mapping in mappings:
   399→        try:
   400→            value_obj = mapping.get("value", {})
   401→            if not isinstance(value_obj, dict):
   402→                continue
   403→
   404→            # Only process standard-type values
   405→            if value_obj.get("type") != "standard":
   406→                continue
   407→
   408→            val_text = value_obj.get("value")
   409→            if not val_text or not isinstance(val_text, str):
   410→                continue
   411→
   412→            # Skip numeric values — they don't need concept grounding
   413→            if numeric_re.match(val_text.strip()):
   414→                continue
   415→
   416→            # Already grounded
   417→            if mapping.get("value_concept_id"):
   418→                continue
   419→
   420→            # Try unit_normalizer's categorical value lookup (fast path)
   421→            normalized_text, concept_id = normalize_value(val_text)
   422→            if concept_id is not None:
   423→                mapping["value_concept_id"] = str(concept_id)
   424→                mapping["value_concept_system"] = "OMOP"
   425→                logger.debug(
   426→                    "Grounded categorical value '%s' → concept_id=%s",
   427→                    val_text,
   428→                    concept_id,
   429→                )
   430→        except Exception as e:
   431→            logger.debug("Categorical value grounding failed for mapping: %s", e)
   432→
   433→    return mappings
   434→
   435→
   436→async def _ground_entity_parallel(
   437→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   438→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   439→    criterion_text: str,
   440→    entity_num: int,
   441→    total: int,
   442→    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
   443→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None, float, int]:
   444→    """Ground a single entity with concurrency control and timing.
   445→
   446→    Acquires the semaphore before grounding to cap concurrent API calls.
   447→    Returns a (result, error) tuple for error isolation -- one entity failure
   448→    does not crash others in the asyncio.gather batch.
   449→
   450→    Args:
   451→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   452→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   453→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   454→        entity_num: 1-based index for logging.
   455→        total: Total entity count for logging.
   456→        semaphore: Concurrency limiter (Semaphore(4)).
   457→
   458→    Returns:
   459→        (EntityGroundingResult, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts) on success,
   460→        or (None, error_message, elapsed_ms, 0) on failure.
   461→    """
   462→    async with semaphore:
   463→        entity_text = entity.get("text", "")[:50]
   464→        entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   465→        start = time.monotonic()
   466→        logger.info(
   467→            "Grounding entity %d/%d: '%s' (type=%s) — start",
   468→            entity_num,
   469→            total,
   470→            entity_text,
   471→            entity_type,
   472→        )
   473→        try:
   474→            # Dual grounding: run TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper
   475→            # in parallel per entity (Phase 1a)
   476→            full_entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   477→            acronym = _is_likely_acronym(full_entity_text)
   478→            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
   479→            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(
   480→                full_entity_text, entity_type, is_acronym=acronym
   481→            )
   482→            (result, retry_attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(
   483→                tu_task, omop_task
   484→            )
   485→
   486→            # Reconcile dual grounding results
   487→            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
   488→
   489→            # Thread criterion_id from parse into result
   490→            criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id")
   491→            if criterion_id:
   492→                result.criterion_id = criterion_id
   493→
   494→            # Generate field mappings (same semaphore slot)
   495→            field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(result, criterion_text)
   496→
   497→            # Post-process: ground categorical values in field mappings
   498→            if field_mappings:
   499→                field_mappings = _ground_categorical_values(field_mappings)
   500→
   501→            result.field_mappings = field_mappings if field_mappings else None
   502→
   503→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   504→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   505→            logger.info(
   506→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' grounded in %.1fs: code=%s, omop=%s, conf=%.2f",
   507→                entity_num,
   508→                total,
   509→                entity_text,
   510→                elapsed,
   511→                result.selected_code or "N/A",
   512→                result.omop_concept_id or "N/A",
   513→                result.confidence,
   514→            )
   515→            return (result, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts)
   516→        except Exception as e:
   517→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   518→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   519→            logger.error(
   520→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' failed in %.1fs: %s",
   521→                entity_num,
   522→                total,
   523→                entity_text,
   524→                elapsed,
   525→                e,
   526→            )
   527→            return (None, str(e), elapsed_ms, 0)
   528→
   529→
   530→async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   531→    """Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.
   532→
   533→    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   534→    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for
   535→    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded
   536→    entity.
   537→
   538→    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in
   539→    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total
   540→    failure to parse entities_json triggers a fatal error.
   541→
   542→    Args:
   543→        state: Current pipeline state with entities_json and protocol_id.
   544→
   545→    Returns:
   546→        Dict with grounded_entities_json and accumulated errors list,
   547→        or fatal error dict if entities_json cannot be parsed.
   548→    """
   549→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
   550→
   551→    if state.get("error"):
   552→        return {}
   553→
   554→    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
   555→    with pipeline_span(
   556→        "ground_node", span_type="TOOL", protocol_id=protocol_id
   557→    ) as span:
   558→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
   559→
   560→        try:
   561→            entities_json = state.get("entities_json")
   562→            if not entities_json:
   563→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No entities_json in state"})
   564→                return {
   565→                    "error": "No entities_json in state — parse node may have failed",
   566→                }
   567→
   568→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = json.loads(entities_json)
   569→            protocol_id = state["protocol_id"]
   570→
   571→            span.set_inputs(
   572→                {
   573→                    "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   574→                    "entity_count": len(entity_items),
   575→                }
   576→            )
   577→
   578→            logger.info(
   579→                "Starting grounding for protocol %s: %d entities",
   580→                protocol_id,
   581→                len(entity_items),
   582→            )
   583→
   584→            # Fail fast: verify OMOP vocabulary is reachable before
   585→            # starting entity grounding. No silent fallback.
   586→            _get_omop_engine()
   587→
   588→            router = _get_router()
   589→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
   590→            grounding_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   591→
   592→            # Pre-process: handle consent entities synchronously (skip grounding)
   593→            entities_to_ground: list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]] = []
   594→            for idx, entity in enumerate(entity_items, 1):
   595→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   596→                entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   597→
   598→                if entity_type == "Consent":
   599→                    logger.info(
   600→                        "Entity '%s' (type=Consent) skipped — consent/participation"
   601→                        " criteria are not groundable to terminology codes",
   602→                        entity_text[:50],
   603→                    )
   604→                    consent_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   605→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   606→                        entity_type=entity_type,
   607→                        selected_code=None,
   608→                        selected_system=None,
   609→                        preferred_term=None,
   610→                        confidence=0.0,
   611→                        candidates=[],
   612→                        reasoning=(
   613→                            "Consent/participation criterion — not groundable"
   614→                            " to medical terminology codes"
   615→                        ),
   616→                        criterion_id=entity.get("criterion_id"),
   617→                    )
   618→                    grounding_results.append(consent_result.model_dump())
   619→                else:
   620→                    entities_to_ground.append((idx, entity))
   621→
   622→            # Dev/debug: truncate entity list for faster runs and fewer API calls.
   623→            # DEV_MAX_ENTITIES takes precedence over PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES when set.
   624→            _max_entities = int(
   625→                os.getenv("DEV_MAX_ENTITIES")
   626→                or os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES")
   627→                or "0"
   628→            )
   629→            if _max_entities > 0:
   630→                entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]
   631→                logger.info(
   632→                    "Max entities limit=%d: truncated to %d entities",
   633→                    _max_entities,
   634→                    len(entities_to_ground),
   635→                )
   636→
   637→            # Pre-flight warmup: absorb MedGemma cold-start latency before
   638→            # the parallel batch. Non-fatal — if warmup fails, entities proceed.
   639→            try:
   640→                from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage as HumanMessageType
   641→
   642→                from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
   643→                    _get_medgemma_model,
   644→                )
   645→
   646→                warmup_model = _get_medgemma_model()
   647→                await warmup_model.ainvoke([HumanMessageType(content="ready")])
   648→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup succeeded")
   649→            except Exception as warmup_err:
   650→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup skipped: %s", warmup_err)
   651→
   652→            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore
   653→            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop
   654→            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
   655→
   656→            tasks = [
   657→                _ground_entity_parallel(
   658→                    entity,
   659→                    router,
   660→                    entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", ""),
   661→                    idx,
   662→                    len(entity_items),
   663→                    semaphore,
   664→                )
   665→                for idx, entity in entities_to_ground
   666→            ]
   667→
   668→            outcomes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
   669→
   670→            # Process outcomes and collect audit entries for batch write
   671→            pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]] = []
   672→            entity_times: list[float] = []
   673→            total_retry_count = 0
   674→            for (idx, entity), (result, error, elapsed_ms, retries) in zip(
   675→                entities_to_ground, outcomes
   676→            ):
   677→                entity_times.append(elapsed_ms)
   678→                total_retry_count += max(0, retries - 1)  # retries beyond first attempt
   679→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   680→                criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   681→
   682→                if result is not None:
   683→                    pending_audits.append(
   684→                        (criterion_id, entity, result.candidates, result)
   685→                    )
   686→                    grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())
   687→                else:
   688→                    # Entity failed — create expert_review fallback
   689→                    cid_short = criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else "unknown"
   690→                    error_msg = (
   691→                        f"Entity grounding failed for '{entity_text[:50]}'"
   692→                        f" (criterion={cid_short}): {error}"
   693→                    )
   694→                    logger.error(error_msg)
   695→                    accumulated_errors.append(error_msg)
   696→
   697→            # Batch-write all audit log entries in a single session
   698→            _flush_audit_logs(protocol_id, pending_audits)
   699→
   700→            logger.info(
   701→                "Ground node complete for protocol %s: %d grounded, %d errors",
   702→                protocol_id,
   703→                len(grounding_results),
   704→                len(accumulated_errors),
   705→            )
   706→
   707→            span.set_outputs(
   708→                {
   709→                    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   710→                    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   711→                    "avg_entity_ms": (
   712→                        round(sum(entity_times) / len(entity_times))
   713→                        if entity_times
   714→                        else 0
   715→                    ),
   716→                    "max_entity_ms": round(max(entity_times)) if entity_times else 0,
   717→                    "retry_count": total_retry_count,
   718→                }
   719→            )
   720→
   721→            return {
   722→                "grounded_entities_json": json.dumps(grounding_results),
   723→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   724→            }
   725→
   726→        except Exception as e:
   727→            logger.exception(
   728→                "Ground node fatal error for protocol %s: %s",
   729→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   730→                e,
   731→            )
   732→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   733→            return {"error": f"Ground node failed: {e}"}
   734→
```

     1→# Copy to .env and set your values. Do not commit .env.
     2→#
     3→# ElixirTrials supports two MedGemma deployment backends:
     4→#   Option A: Vertex AI Model Garden (cloud, recommended for production)
     5→#   Option B: Local GPU via HuggingFace (requires NVIDIA GPU with 8GB+ VRAM)
     6→
     7→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
     8→# REQUIRED: Gemini (extraction, structuring, ordinal resolution)
     9→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    10→
    11→# Gemini Developer API key (required for all pipelines)
    12→# Get a key at https://aistudio.google.com/apikey
    13→GOOGLE_API_KEY=
    14→
    15→# Gemini model for criteria extraction (default: gemini-2.5-flash)
    16→GEMINI_MODEL_NAME=gemini-2.5-flash
    17→
    18→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    19→# REQUIRED: UMLS (terminology grounding)
    20→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    21→
    22→# Get a key at https://uts.nlm.nih.gov/uts/signup-login
    23→UMLS_API_KEY=
    24→
    25→# Optional UMLS overrides
    26→# UMLS_BASE_URL=https://uts-ws.nlm.nih.gov/rest
    27→# UMLS_CACHE_DIR=
    28→# UMLS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=10
    29→
    30→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    31→# MedGemma Backend — Option A: Vertex AI (recommended)
    32→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    33→
    34→MODEL_BACKEND=vertex
    35→GCP_PROJECT_ID=
    36→GCP_REGION=europe-west4
    37→VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID=
    38→# Alternative: use a named model instead of endpoint ID
    39→# VERTEX_MODEL_NAME=
    40→
    41→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    42→# MedGemma Backend — Option B: Local GPU
    43→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    44→
    45→# Uncomment and set MODEL_BACKEND=local to use local GPU inference
    46→# MODEL_BACKEND=local
    47→# MEDGEMMA_MODEL_PATH=google/medgemma-4b-it
    48→# MEDGEMMA_QUANTIZATION=4bit
    49→# MEDGEMMA_MAX_TOKENS=2048
    50→
    51→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    52→# Optional: Google OAuth (UI login)

    54→
    55→# Create at: https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials
    56→# Redirect URI: http://localhost:8000/auth/callback
    57→GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=

    59→
    60→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    61→# Optional: GCloud / ADC

    63→
    64→# GCloud configuration profile (activated by make run-dev / run-api).
    65→# make run-dev runs gcloud auth login (browser) so you are not prompted for a password.
    66→GCLOUD_PROFILE=
    67→# Quota project for Application Default Credentials
    68→# GOOGLE_CLOUD_QUOTA_PROJECT=
    69→
    70→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    71→# Observability: MLflow
    72→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    74→# When running locally via "make run-dev", MLflow runs on localhost:5001

    77→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    78→# Dev / debug: limit criteria and grounding for faster runs
    79→# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    80→# Limit how many criteria are used from extraction (parse node) and thus how many

     1→"""Pydantic schemas for terminology grounding results and API candidates.
     2→
     3→These schemas define the machine-readable format for agent-to-agent communication
     4→between TerminologyRouter and MedGemma during the grounding phase.

     6→Per user decision: "Agent-to-agent communication must be machine-readable

    10→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    11→
    12→
    13→class GroundingCandidate(BaseModel):
    14→    """A single candidate match from a terminology API.
    15→
    16→    Attributes:
    17→        source_api: Name of the API that produced this candidate.
    18→        code: Terminology code (e.g. UMLS CUI "C0011847", RxNorm "6809").
    19→        preferred_term: Canonical preferred name for the concept.
    20→        semantic_type: UMLS semantic type. None for non-UMLS APIs.
    21→        score: Relevance score from the API (0.0-1.0). Higher is better.
    22→    """
    23→
    24→    source_api: str = Field(
    25→        description="Name of the terminology API that produced this candidate"
    26→    )
    27→    code: str = Field(description="Terminology code (CUI, RxNorm, ICD-10, etc.)")
    28→    preferred_term: str = Field(description="Canonical preferred name for the concept")
    29→    semantic_type: str | None = Field(
    30→        default=None,
    31→        description=(
    32→            "UMLS semantic type (e.g. 'Disease or Syndrome'). None for non-UMLS APIs."
    33→        ),
    34→    )
    35→    score: float = Field(
    36→        ge=0.0,
    37→        le=1.0,
    38→        description="Relevance score from the API (0.0-1.0). Higher is better.",
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→class EntityGroundingResult(BaseModel):
    43→    """Grounding result for a single entity after MedGemma evaluation.
    44→
    45→    Contains the selected code, all candidates considered, and the reasoning

    48→
    49→    Attributes:

    52→        selected_code: The chosen terminology code. None if grounding failed.
    53→        selected_system: The coding system for the selected code.
    54→        preferred_term: Canonical name for the selected code.

import (\n        TerminologyRouter,\n        _is_likely_acronym,\n    )\n\n    router = TerminologyRouter()\n    results = []\n\n    # We don't have pre-extracted entities — we need to extract them first.\n    # Use Gemini to decompose the snippet into entities, then ground each one.\n    from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_extractor import (\n        extract_entities_from_criterion,\n    )\n\n    entities = await extract_entities_from_criterion(snippet[\"snippet_text\"])\n\n    for ent in entities:\n        entity_text = ent.get(\"text\", ent.get(\"entity\", \"\"))\n        entity_type = ent.get(\"entity_type\", \"Condition\")\n\n        entity_dict = {\n            \"text\": entity_text,\n            \"entity_type\": entity_type,\n            \"criterion_text\": snippet[\"snippet_text\"],\n        }\n\n        try:\n            acronym = _is_likely_acronym(entity_text)\n\n            # Parallel: TerminologyRouter + OMOP\n            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(\n                entity_dict, router, snippet[\"snippet_text\"]\n            )\n            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(\n                entity_text, entity_type, is_acronym=acronym\n            )\n            (result, _attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(\n                tu_task, omop_task\n            )\n\n            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)\n\n            # Field mappings\n            field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(\n                result, snippet[\"snippet_text\"]\n            )\n            if field_mappings:\n                field_mappings = _ground_categorical_values(field_mappings)\n            result.field_mappings = field_mappings or None\n\n            results.append(\n                {\n                    \"entity_name\": result.preferred_term or result.entity_text,\n                    \"entity_text\": result.entity_text,\n                    \"entity_type\": entity_type,\n                    \"system\": result.selected_system,\n                    \"code\": result.selected_code,\n                    \"confidence\": result.confidence,\n                    \"omop_id\": result.omop_concept_id,\n                    \"reconciliation\": result.reconciliation_status,\n                    \"field_mappings\": result.field_mappings,\n                }\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            results.append(\n                {\n                    \"entity_name\": entity_text,\n                    \"entity_text\": entity_text,\n                    \"entity_type\": entity_type,\n                    \"error\": str(e),\n                }\n            )\n\n    return {\"snippet_text\": snippet[\"snippet_text\"], \"grounded_entities\": results}\n\n\nasync def ground_snippet_with_known_entities(snippet: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Ground a snippet using its pre-defined golden entities (skip extraction).\"\"\"\n    from protocol_processor.nodes.ground import (\n        _ground_entity_with_retry,\n        _reconcile_dual_grounding,\n    )\n    from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import lookup_omop_concept\n    from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import (\n        TerminologyRouter,\n        _is_likely_acronym,\n    )\n\n    router = TerminologyRouter()\n    results = []\n\n    for expected_entity in snippet[\"entities\"]:\n        entity_text = expected_entity[\"entity_name\"]\n        # Guess entity_type from context\n        entity_type = \"Lab_Value\" if expected_entity[\"relation\"] in (\"<\", \">\", \"<=\", \">=\") else \"Condition\"\n\n        entity_dict = {\n            \"text\": entity_text,\n            \"entity_type\": entity_type,\n            \"criterion_text\": snippet[\"snippet_text\"],\n        }\n\n        try:\n            acronym = _is_likely_acronym(entity_text)\n\n            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(\n                entity_dict, router, snippet[\"snippet_text\"]\n            )\n            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(\n                entity_text, entity_type, is_acronym=acronym\n            )\n            (result, _attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(\n                tu_task, omop_task\n            )\n\n            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)\n\n            results.append(\n                {\n                    \"entity_name\": result.preferred_term or result.entity_text,\n                    \"entity_text\": result.entity_text,\n                    \"system\": result.selected_system,\n                    \"code\": result.selected_code,\n                    \"confidence\": result.confidence,\n                    \"omop_id\": result.omop_concept_id,\n                    \"reconciliation\": result.reconciliation_status,\n                    \"expected_code\": expected_entity[\"code\"],\n                    \"expected_system\": expected_entity[\"system\"],\n                    \"code_match\": (\n                        result.selected_code == expected_entity[\"code\"]\n                        if result.selected_code\n                        else False\n                    ),\n                }\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            results.append(\n                {\n                    \"entity_name\": entity_text,\n                    \"error\": str(e),\n                    \"expected_code\": expected_entity[\"code\"],\n                    \"code_match\": False,\n                }\n            )\n\n    return {\"snippet_text\": snippet[\"snippet_text\"], \"grounded_entities\": results}\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Pretty-print results\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef print_results(all_results: list[dict]) -> None:\n    total_entities = 0\n    matched = 0\n    grounded = 0\n\n    for i, res in enumerate(all_results):\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n        snippet = res[\"snippet_text\"]\n        print(f\"Snippet {i+1}: {snippet[:100]}{'...' if len(snippet) > 100 else ''}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n\n        for ent in res[\"grounded_entities\"]:\n            total_entities += 1\n            code = ent.get(\"code\")\n            expected = ent.get(\"expected_code\", \"?\")\n            match = ent.get(\"code_match\", False)\n            conf = ent.get(\"confidence\", 0)\n            err = ent.get(\"error\")\n\n            if code:\n                grounded += 1\n            if match:\n                matched += 1\n\n            status = \"MATCH\" if match else (\"MISS\" if code else \"FAIL\")\n            icon = \"\\033[32m✓\\033[0m\" if match else (\"\\033[33m≈\\033[0m\" if code else \"\\033[31m✗\\033[0m\")\n\n            if err:\n                print(f\"  {icon} [{status}] {ent['entity_name']}\")\n                print(f\"      ERROR: {err}\")\n            else:\n                name = ent.get(\"entity_name\", \"?\")\n                system = ent.get(\"system\", \"?\")\n                omop = ent.get(\"omop_id\", \"—\")\n                recon = ent.get(\"reconciliation\", \"—\")\n                print(f\"  {icon} [{status}] {name}\")\n                print(f\"      Got:      {system}:{code}  (conf={conf:.2f}, omop={omop}, recon={recon})\")\n                print(f\"      Expected: {ent.get('expected_system', '?')}:{expected}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"  Total entities:  {total_entities}\")\n    print(f\"  Grounded:        {grounded}/{total_entities}\")\n    print(f\"  Exact code match: {matched}/{total_entities}\")\n    print(\n        f\"  Accuracy:        {matched/total_entities*100:.0f}%\"\n        if total_entities\n        else \"  Accuracy:        N/A\"\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Main\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nasync def main() -> None:\n    print(\"Grounding Test Snippets — Live Pipeline Evaluation\")\n    print(f\"Running {len(grounding_snippets)} snippets with known entities...\\n\")\n\n    results = []\n    for i, snippet in enumerate(grounding_snippets):\n        entity_count = len(snippet[\"entities\"])\n        print(\n            f\"[{i+1}/{len(grounding_snippets)}] \"\n            f\"Grounding {entity_count} entities from: \"\n            f\"{snippet['snippet_text'][:70]}...\"\n        )\n        result = await ground_snippet_with_known_entities(snippet)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    print_results(results)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(main())\n"
}

Here are the live grounding results against the 5 golden snippets:

## Results: 10/10 entities grounded, 5/10 exact code match (50%)

| # | Snippet | Entity | Got | Expected | Match |
|---|---------|--------|-----|----------|-------|
| 1 | Serum creatinine >1.5x ULN | Serum creatinine level | `C0600061` | `C0201975` | MISS — got the substance, expected the lab test |
| 2 | eGFR <45 ml/min | Estimated GFR decreased | `C4552444` | `C0858118` | MISS — got "decreased" variant, expected base concept |
| 3 | Body weight <50 kg | Body Weight | `C0005910` | `C0005910` | **MATCH** |
| 3 | BMI >44 kg/m2 | Body mass index | `C1305855` | `C1305855` | **MATCH** |
| 4 | Active AS | Juvenile ankylosing spondylitis | `C0409675` | `C0038013` | MISS — got juvenile subtype vs general AS |
| 4 | Radiologic evidence | Diagnostic radiologic exam | `C0043299` | `C0034693` | MISS — slightly different CUI for radiology |
| 5 | Male subjects | Male Gender | `C1706180` | `C1706180` | **MATCH** |
| 5 | Female subjects | Female Phenotype | `C1705498` | `C1705498` | **MATCH** |
| 5 | Diagnosis of PD | Parkinson Disease | `C0030567` | `C0030567` | **MATCH** |
| 5 | GBA mutation | GBA gene mutation | `C3888963` | `C4225361` | MISS — different CUI for GBA gene concept |

**Key observations:**
- **100% grounding rate** — every entity got a code (none failed)
- **All entities got OMOP reconciliation** — 9/10 "agree" between UMLS and OMOP, 1 "omop_missing" (GBA)
- **Confidence is high** — 0.90-1.00 across the board
- The 5 misses are **semantically close** — they're related UMLS concepts, not wrong answers (e.g. "Serum creatinine level" vs "Serum creatinine measurement", "Juvenile AS" vs general "AS")
