  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Ground the new test snippets (no expected values) and report results.\n\nUsage:\n    set -a && source .env && set +a\n    uv run python scripts/test_new_snippets.py\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSNIPPETS_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"tests\" / \"e2e\" / \"test_snippets.json\"\ndata = json.loads(SNIPPETS_PATH.read_text())\ngrounding_snippets = data[\"grounding_test_snippets\"]\n\n# Only the new ones (empty entities)\nNEW_SNIPPETS = [s for s in grounding_snippets if len(s[\"entities\"]) == 0]\n\n\nasync def ground_snippet(snippet: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Ground using the entity names from the snippet via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma + OMOP.\"\"\"\n    from protocol_processor.nodes.ground import (\n        _ground_entity_with_retry,\n        _reconcile_dual_grounding,\n    )\n    from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import lookup_omop_concept\n    from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import (\n        TerminologyRouter,\n        _is_likely_acronym,\n    )\n    from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_extractor import (\n        extract_entities_from_criterion,\n    )\n    from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import generate_field_mappings\n    from protocol_processor.nodes.ground import _ground_categorical_values\n\n    router = TerminologyRouter()\n    results = []\n\n    # Step 1: Extract entities from snippet text using Gemini\n    print(f\"  Extracting entities...\")\n    entities = await extract_entities_from_criterion(snippet[\"snippet_text\"])\n    print(f\"  Found {len(entities)} entities: {[e.get('text', e.get('entity', '?')) for e in entities]}\")\n\n    # Step 2: Ground each entity\n    for ent in entities:\n        entity_text = ent.get(\"text\", ent.get(\"entity\", \"\"))\n        entity_type = ent.get(\"entity_type\", \"Condition\")\n\n        entity_dict = {\n            \"text\": entity_text,\n            \"entity_type\": entity_type,\n            \"criterion_text\": snippet[\"snippet_text\"],\n        }\n\n        try:\n            acronym = _is_likely_acronym(entity_text)\n            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity_dict, router, snippet[\"snippet_text\"])\n            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(entity_text, entity_type, is_acronym=acronym)\n            (result, _attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(tu_task, omop_task)\n            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)\n\n            # Field mappings\n            field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(result, snippet[\"snippet_text\"])\n            if field_mappings:\n                field_mappings = _ground_categorical_values(field_mappings)\n            result.field_mappings = field_mappings or None\n\n            fm_summary = []\n            if result.field_mappings:\n                for fm in result.field_mappings:\n                    v = fm.get(\"value\", {})\n                    val_str = v.get(\"value\", \"\") if isinstance(v, dict) else str(v)\n                    unit = v.get(\"unit\", \"\") if isinstance(v, dict) else \"\"\n                    rel = fm.get(\"relation\", \"\")\n                    fm_summary.append(f\"{rel} {val_str} {unit}\".strip())\n\n            results.append({\n                \"entity_name\": result.preferred_term or result.entity_text,\n                \"entity_text\": result.entity_text,\n                \"entity_type\": entity_type,\n                \"system\": result.selected_system,\n                \"code\": result.selected_code,\n                \"confidence\": result.confidence,\n                \"omop_id\": result.omop_concept_id,\n                \"reconciliation\": result.reconciliation_status,\n                \"field_mappings_summary\": fm_summary,\n            })\n        except Exception as e:\n            results.append({\n                \"entity_name\": entity_text,\n                \"entity_type\": entity_type,\n                \"error\": str(e),\n            })\n\n    return {\"snippet_text\": snippet[\"snippet_text\"], \"grounded_entities\": results}\n\n\ndef print_results(all_results: list[dict]) -> None:\n    for i, res in enumerate(all_results):\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n        print(f\"Snippet: {res['snippet_text']}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n\n        for ent in res[\"grounded_entities\"]:\n            err = ent.get(\"error\")\n            if err:\n                print(f\"  \\033[31m✗\\033[0m {ent['entity_name']} ({ent.get('entity_type','?')})\")\n                print(f\"      ERROR: {err}\")\n                continue\n\n            name = ent[\"entity_name\"]\n            system = ent.get(\"system\", \"?\")\n            code = ent.get(\"code\", \"?\")\n            conf = ent.get(\"confidence\", 0)\n            omop = ent.get(\"omop_id\", \"—\")\n            recon = ent.get(\"reconciliation\", \"—\")\n            etype = ent.get(\"entity_type\", \"?\")\n            fm = ent.get(\"field_mappings_summary\", [])\n\n            icon = \"\\033[32m✓\\033[0m\" if code else \"\\033[31m✗\\033[0m\"\n            print(f\"  {icon} {name}\")\n            print(f\"      type={etype}  code={system}:{code}  conf={conf:.2f}\")\n            print(f\"      omop={omop}  reconciliation={recon}\")\n            if fm:\n                print(f\"      field_mappings: {fm}\")\n\n        if not res[\"grounded_entities\"]:\n            print(\"  (no entities extracted)\")\n\n\nasync def main() -> None:\n    print(\"Grounding New Snippets — Full Extraction + Grounding\")\n    print(f\"Running {len(NEW_SNIPPETS)} new snippets...\\n\")\n\n    results = []\n    for i, snippet in enumerate(NEW_SNIPPETS):\n        print(f\"[{i+1}/{len(NEW_SNIPPETS)}] {snippet['snippet_text']}\")\n        result = await ground_snippet(snippet)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    print_results(results)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(main())\n"
}

## Comprehensive Analysis: Grounding Pipeline for Medical Entities

Based on my thorough examination of the codebase, here's how "surgically sterile" would trace through the grounding pipeline:

### 1. ENTITY DECOMPOSITION STAGE

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/entity_decomposer.py`

**Prompt Template**: `entity_decompose.jinja2`

When a criterion text like "Must be surgically sterile" is processed, the entity decomposer:
- Uses Gemini 2.5 Flash with structured output to extract discrete medical entities
- Applies specific extraction rules defined in the prompt (see below)
- Returns a list of `DecomposedEntity` objects with `text` and `entity_type`

**Key Extraction Rules**:
```
- Extract the medical ENTITY (not the full sentence)
- Skip non-groundable concepts: consent, willingness, investigator discretion, geographic location
- One criterion may yield 0, 1, or multiple entities
- Entity types: Condition | Medication | Lab_Value | Procedure | Demographic | Other
```

**For "surgically sterile"**:
- The decomposer would attempt to extract the medical concept
- This phrase is likely classified as a non-groundable criterion (similar to "consent", "willingness", or other participation/eligibility criteria that are administrative rather than clinical)
- Possible outcomes:
  - Returns empty list (0 entities) → no grounding needed
  - Returns `{"text": "sterile", "entity_type": "Other"}` → routes to expert_review since "Other" has no APIs configured

---

### 2. TERMINOLOGY ROUTING STAGE

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/terminology_router.py`

**Configuration**: `config/routing.yaml`

The router makes routing decisions based on entity type:

**Routing Logic**:
```python
def route_entity(entity_text, entity_type):
    1. Get APIs for entity_type from routing.yaml
    2. Check if text is a likely acronym (≤6 chars, all uppercase, no spaces)
    3. If acronym, add UMLS to the API list
    4. Query all matching APIs via ToolUniverse
    5. Return list of GroundingCandidate objects from all APIs
```

**Routing Configuration for Key Types**:
```yaml
Medication: [rxnorm, umls]
Condition: [icd10, snomed, umls]
Lab_Value: [loinc, umls]
Procedure: [umls, snomed]
Demographic: [umls, snomed]
Consent: {skip: true}  # Explicitly skipped
Other: []  # No APIs configured → expert_review
```

**For "surgically sterile"**:
- If classified as "Other": No APIs queried → returns empty candidate list
- If somehow classified as "Condition": Would query ICD-10, SNOMED, UMLS
- But realistically, it's non-medical vocabulary and would not match meaningfully in any system

**The `_is_likely_acronym()` Function**:
```python
def _is_likely_acronym(text: str) -> bool:
    """Check if text is likely a medical acronym.
    
    Returns True if:
    - Text is ≤6 chars
    - All uppercase
    - No spaces
    
    Pure string check — zero cost.
    """
    stripped = text.strip()
    if not stripped or " " in stripped:
        return False
    if len(stripped) > 6:
        return False
    return stripped.upper() == stripped
```

---

### 3. OMOP VOCABULARY LOOKUP (Parallel with TerminologyRouter)

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py`

The OMOP mapper runs **in parallel** with TerminologyRouter:

**Lookup Process**:
```python
async def lookup_omop_concept(
    entity_text: str,
    entity_type: str,
    is_acronym: bool = False
) -> OmopLookupResult:
    1. Map entity_type to OMOP domain(s)
    2. Query concept table (concept_name ILIKE '%entity_text%')
    3. Query concept_synonym table (synonym_name ILIKE '%entity_text%')
    4. Deduplicate candidates by concept_id
    5. Score using difflib.SequenceMatcher ratio + bonuses
    6. Apply primary domain bonus (+0.05)
    7. Return best candidate if score ≥ OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE (0.3)
```

**Entity Type to OMOP Domain Mapping**:
```python
ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAINS = {
    "Condition": ["Condition", "Observation"],  # primary, fallback
    "Medication": ["Drug"],
    "Lab_Value": ["Measurement", "Observation"],
    "Procedure": ["Procedure", "Observation"],
    "Demographic": ["Observation", "Measurement"],
}
```

**Scoring Algorithm**:
- Base score = `difflib.SequenceMatcher.ratio(entity_text, match_text)`
- Bonus for substring containment: +0.15
- Bonus for exact match: +0.25
- Primary domain bonus: +0.05
- Final score capped at 1.0

**For "surgically sterile"**:
- Would search OMOP vocabularies with ILIKE pattern `%surgically sterile%`
- Unlikely to find any standard OMOP concept
- Would return empty `OmopLookupResult` (all fields None)

### 4. MEDGEMMA DECISION STAGE

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/medgemma_decider.py`

**Process**:
```python
async def medgemma_decide(
    entity: dict,
    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
    criterion_context: str
) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    1. If no candidates:
       - Return EntityGroundingResult with confidence=0.0, no code
    2. Call MedGemma with system prompt + evaluation prompt
    3. MedGemma analyzes candidates and selects best match
    4. Gemini structures the raw MedGemma output
    5. Return EntityGroundingResult with selected code, confidence, reasoning
```

**System Prompt** (`grounding_system.jinja2`):
- Defines confidence scoring rules (0.9-1.0 exact, 0.7-0.8 synonym, 0.5-0.6 partial, 0.0 no match)
- Rules for preferring specific systems (RxNorm > UMLS for meds, SNOMED for conditions)
- Search strategy for retry attempts (simplify, drop qualifiers, expand abbreviations)

**Evaluation Prompt** (`grounding_evaluate.jinja2`):
- Shows entity text, type, criterion context, candidates list
- Asks MedGemma to:
  1. Select best candidate or state none exists
  2. Assign confidence score
  3. Provide brief reasoning

**For "surgically sterile"**:
- If no candidates from TerminologyRouter and OMOP: Returns empty result (confidence=0.0)
- If somehow candidates exist: MedGemma would likely select confidence=0.0 (not a medical concept)

### 5. AGENTIC RETRY LOOP (If confidence < 0.5)

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`

**Retry Logic**:
```python
async def _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text):
    # Step 1: Initial routing + MedGemma decision
    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
    
    # Step 2-3: Retry loop (max 3 attempts total)
    attempt = 1
    while (
        result.selected_code is None
        and result.confidence < 0.5
        and attempt < 3
    ):
        attempt += 1
        # Ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions:
        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(...)
        
        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
            # Skip this entity → mark as skipped
            return EntityGroundingResult(skipped=True, ...)
        
        # Determine new query from:
        # - rephrased_query
        # - derived_term
        # - gemini_suggestion
        # - original query
        new_query = reasoning_result.rephrased_query or ...
        
        # Retry with new query
        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
        if new_candidates:
            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, ...)
            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
                return (result, attempt)
    
    # Step 4: If all attempts exhausted
    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
        return (result.with_expert_review_marker(), attempt)
```

**The 3 Reasoning Questions** (`grounding_reasoning.jinja2`):

1. **Is it a valid medical criterion?**
   - Skip if: consent, participation, willingness, investigator discretion, geographic location
   - For "surgically sterile": NOT a valid medical criterion → `should_skip=true`

2. **Is it a derived entity mapping to a standard concept?**
   - Example: "age >= 18 years" → derived concept is "age"
   - For "surgically sterile": NOT derived

3. **Can it be rephrased for better search?**
   - Strategies: expand abbreviations, drop qualifiers, use core noun
   - For "surgically sterile": Cannot be usefully rephrased as medical terminology

**For "surgically sterile"** in the retry loop:
- Attempt 1 fails (no candidates or confidence < 0.5)
- Agentic reasoning detects `should_skip=true` → entity marked as skipped immediately
- Returns early without further retry attempts

### 6. DUAL GROUNDING RECONCILIATION

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`

**Reconciliation Function**:
```python
def _reconcile_dual_grounding(
    result: EntityGroundingResult,  # from TerminologyRouter
    omop_result: OmopLookupResult,   # from OMOP mapper
) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    """Reconcile TU + OMOP results into single result with status."""
    
    tu_ok = result.selected_code is not None
    omop_ok = omop_result.omop_concept_id is not None
    
    if tu_ok and omop_ok:
        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
        # Fuzzy match check (token_set_ratio >= 80%)
        if fuzzy_match(result.preferred_term, omop_result.omop_concept_name):
            result.reconciliation_status = "agree"
        else:
            result.reconciliation_status = "disagreement"
    elif tu_ok and not omop_ok:
        result.reconciliation_status = "omop_missing"
    elif not tu_ok and omop_ok:
        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
        result.reconciliation_status = "tooluniverse_missing"
    # else: neither — leave as-is (expert_review path)
    
    return result
```

**Reconciliation Statuses**:
- `"agree"`: Both found same concept (fuzzy match ≥ 80%)
- `"disagreement"`: Both found different concepts
- `"omop_missing"`: Only TerminologyRouter found match
- `"tooluniverse_missing"`: Only OMOP found match
- `None`: Neither found match (expert_review)

**For "surgically sterile"**:
- Both TU and OMOP return empty results
- Reconciliation status = `None` (or could be marked for expert_review)

### 7. FIELD MAPPING GENERATION

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`

**Process**:
```python
async def generate_field_mappings(
    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
    criterion_text: str
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Generate AutoCriteria field mappings: Entity-Relation-Value-Unit pattern."""
    
    # Uses Gemini structured output
    prompt = f"""
    Medical entity: {entity.preferred_term} (grounded to {entity.selected_code})
    Criterion text: {criterion_text}
    
    Decompose into:
    - entity: specific measurement (e.g., 'HbA1c')
    - relation: operator (=, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, contains, etc.)
    - value: typed object with type discriminator:
      * standard: {"type": "standard", "value": "7", "unit": "%"}
      * range: {"type": "range", "min": "18", "max": "65", "unit": "years"}
      * temporal: {"type": "temporal", "duration": "6", "unit": "months"}
    - unit: measurement unit (%, mg/dL, years, months)
    - value_concept_id: OMOP concept ID for categorical values (optional)
    """
    
    return [
        {
            "entity": m.entity,
            "relation": m.relation,
            "value": m.value.model_dump(exclude_none=True),
            "entity_code": entity.selected_code,
            "entity_system": entity.selected_system,
            "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
            "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
            "value_concept_id": m.value_concept_id,
            "value_concept_system": m.value_concept_system,
        }
    ]
```

**For "surgically sterile"**:
- If entity grounding failed (no code): Field mapping generation skipped or returns empty list
- No field mappings generated

### 8. PARALLEL EXECUTION & CONCURRENCY

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`

**Parallel Execution Pattern**:
```python
async def _ground_entity_parallel(
    entity: dict,
    router: TerminologyRouter,
    criterion_text: str,
    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None, float, int]:
    async with semaphore:  # Cap concurrency at 4 simultaneous entities
        # Phase 1a: Dual grounding in parallel
        tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
        omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(full_entity_text, entity_type, is_acronym)
        (result, retry_attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(tu_task, omop_task)
        
        # Phase 1b: Reconcile dual results
        result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
        
        # Phase 2: Generate field mappings (same semaphore slot)
        field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(result, criterion_text)
        
        # Phase 3: Ground categorical values in field mappings
        field_mappings = _ground_categorical_values(field_mappings)
        
        return (result, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts)
```

**Semaphore Control**: Cap concurrent API calls at 4 entities simultaneously to avoid overwhelming external APIs

### KEY INSIGHTS

1. **Entity Decomposition is Critical**: The prompt in `entity_decompose.jinja2` explicitly lists non-groundable concepts. "Surgically sterile" is not a medical concept like "sterility" or "infertility"—it's an eligibility criterion about surgical status.

2. **Routing Configuration is Centralized**: All entity type → API mappings are in `routing.yaml`. "Other" type entities have no APIs configured, triggering expert_review routing.

3. **Dual Grounding is Parallel**: TerminologyRouter and OMOP mapper run simultaneously for speed, then reconcile results with fuzzy matching (token_set_ratio ≥ 80%).

4. **Agentic Retry is Smart**: The retry loop asks 3 reasoning questions before retrying, allowing it to:
   - Skip non-medical entities early
   - Map derived entities ("age >= 18" → "age")
   - Rephrase queries (expand abbreviations, drop qualifiers)

5. **Error Accumulation by Design**: Individual entity failures don't crash the pipeline. Errors are logged and accumulated, with partial results returned alongside successes.

6. **Audit Trail**: Every grounding decision (success or failure) is logged to `AuditLog` with full context for human review.

    17→5. The `value` field is NOT used by this tool. Only extract `text` (the medical concept) and `entity_type`.
    18→6. Extract entities even from indication or context-framing criteria. The sentence structure does not matter — extract every medical concept regardless of how it is phrased.
    19→7. Any clinical scoring system, classification scale, physical status grade, or ordinal assessment is Lab_Value — never "Other". This includes ALL scales you know of, whether common or rare.
    20→8. Use "Other" ONLY as a last resort for truly unclassifiable entities. Always prefer a specific type. If in doubt between "Other" and a specific type, choose the specific type.
    21→
    22→## Compound Concept Rules — DO NOT Over-Decompose
    23→- Do NOT separate anatomical modifiers, severities, laterality, or procedure types from their parent concept. These are a single medical entity:
    24→  - "tricompartmental knee replacement" → ONE entity (not "knee replacement" + "tricompartmental")
    25→  - "spinal muscular atrophy" → ONE entity
    26→  - "non-small cell lung cancer" → ONE entity
    27→  - "left ventricular hypertrophy" → ONE entity
    28→  - "chronic obstructive pulmonary disease" → ONE entity
    29→  - "bilateral total hip arthroplasty" → ONE entity
    30→- DO separate distinct comorbidities or co-occurring conditions into individual entities:
    31→  - "Type 2 diabetes with nephropathy" → TWO entities: "type 2 diabetes" AND "nephropathy"
    32→  - "Hypertension and heart failure" → TWO entities: "hypertension" AND "heart failure"
    33→  - "Asthma or COPD" → TWO entities: "asthma" AND "chronic obstructive pulmonary disease"
    34→
    35→## Criterion
    36→{% if category %}Category hint: {{ category }}{% endif %}
    37→Text: "{{ criterion_text }}"
    38→
    39→Return a JSON object with key "entities" containing an array of {"text": string, "entity_type": string} objects.
    40→If no groundable entities exist, return {"entities": []}.
    41→
```

    10→# Note: Demographic entities are NOT skipped — age/gender grounding is attempted

    17→terminology code? Examples of entities that are NOT groundable: consent, participation,
    18→willingness, study enrollment, investigator discretion, geographic location.
    19→If it is not a valid medical criterion, return should_skip=true.
    20→
    21→**Q2: Derived entity?**
    22→Is "{{ entity_text }}" a derived entity that maps to a more standard medical concept?
    23→Examples:
    24→- "age >= 18 years" → the concept is "age" (the numeric value is a filter, not the concept)
    25→- "male" or "female" → maps to SNOMED gender concepts
    26→- "BMI > 30" → the concept is "body mass index"
    27→If derived, return is_derived=true and the standard medical concept term.
    28→
    29→**Q3: Rephrase for better terminology search?**
    30→Can "{{ entity_text }}" be rephrased as proper medical terminology to improve search results?
    31→{% if previous_query and previous_query != entity_text %}
    32→IMPORTANT: The previous search for "{{ previous_query }}" returned 0 results. You MUST suggest a SUBSTANTIALLY DIFFERENT, SIMPLER query — not a minor variation of the same phrase.
    33→{% endif %}
    34→
    35→Simplification strategies (try in order):
    36→1. Expand abbreviations/acronyms: "HTN" → "hypertension", "MI" → "myocardial infarction", "DM" → "diabetes mellitus", "CKD" → "chronic kidney disease"
    37→2. Use standard medical terminology: "high blood pressure" → "hypertension", "sugar diabetes" → "diabetes mellitus", "heart attack" → "myocardial infarction"
    38→3. Drop qualifiers to broaden: "severe persistent asthma" → "asthma", "bilateral knee osteoarthritis" → "osteoarthritis"
    39→4. Use the core medical noun only: "left ventricular ejection fraction" → "ejection fraction"
    40→
    41→If rephrasing would help, return the rephrased query. Otherwise return null.

    43→## Instructions

    45→Respond with a brief clinical analysis. Address all three questions.
    46→State whether the entity should be skipped, whether it is a derived entity (and the

```
     1→"""Entity decomposition: extract discrete medical entities from criteria.
     2→
     3→Criterion sentences like "eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2 or sCr <= 2.0 mg/dL" contain
     4→multiple groundable medical concepts. This tool decomposes them into discrete
     5→entities with correct types for TerminologyRouter dispatch.
     6→
     7→Uses the two-model architecture pattern: Gemini for structured output via
     8→LangChain's with_structured_output, matching the medgemma_decider.py pattern.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→from typing import Literal, cast
    17→
    18→from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader
    19→from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    20→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→PROMPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "prompts"
    25→
    26→
    27→class DecomposedEntity(BaseModel):
    28→    """A single medical entity extracted from a criterion sentence.
    29→
    30→    Attributes:
    31→        text: The specific medical term to ground (e.g. "eGFR", not the full sentence).
    32→        entity_type: Entity type matching routing.yaml keys exactly.
    33→    """
    34→
    35→    text: str = Field(description="The specific medical term to ground")
    36→    entity_type: Literal[
    37→        "Condition", "Medication", "Lab_Value", "Procedure", "Demographic", "Other"
    38→    ] = Field(description="Entity type for terminology routing")
    39→
    40→
    41→class DecomposedEntityList(BaseModel):
    42→    """List of decomposed entities from a single criterion sentence."""
    43→
    44→    entities: list[DecomposedEntity] = Field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→
    47→def _render_decompose_prompt(criterion_text: str, category: str | None) -> str:
    48→    """Render the entity decomposition Jinja2 prompt template.
    49→
    50→    Args:
    51→        criterion_text: Full criterion sentence to decompose.
    52→        category: Optional category hint from extraction (e.g. "lab_values").
    53→
    54→    Returns:
    55→        Rendered prompt string for Gemini.
    56→    """
    57→    env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader(str(PROMPTS_DIR)), autoescape=False)
    58→    template = env.get_template("entity_decompose.jinja2")
    59→    return template.render(criterion_text=criterion_text, category=category or "")
    60→
    61→
    62→async def decompose_entities_from_criterion(
    63→    criterion_text: str,
    64→    category: str | None,
    65→) -> list[dict]:
    66→    """Extract discrete medical entities from a criterion sentence.
    67→
    68→    Uses Gemini with structured output to decompose a criterion sentence
    69→    into individual medical terms with correct entity types. Falls back
    70→    to empty list on failure -- caller handles fallback to full-text entity.
    71→
    72→    Args:
    73→        criterion_text: The full criterion sentence to decompose.
    74→        category: Optional category hint from extraction.
    75→
    76→    Returns:
    77→        List of dicts with "text" and "entity_type" keys, or empty list on failure.
    78→    """
    79→    try:
    80→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    81→            model=os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash"),
    82→            google_api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
    83→        )
    84→        structured = gemini.with_structured_output(DecomposedEntityList)
    85→        prompt = _render_decompose_prompt(criterion_text, category)
    86→        result = await structured.ainvoke(prompt)
    87→        if isinstance(result, dict):
    88→            result = DecomposedEntityList.model_validate(result)
    89→        decomposed = cast(DecomposedEntityList, result)
    90→        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]
    91→    except Exception as e:
    92→        logger.error(
    93→            "Entity decomposition failed for criterion '%s': %s",
    94→            criterion_text[:80],
    95→            e,
    96→        )
    97→        return []  # Caller falls back to full-text entity
    98→

     1→"""Ground node: route entities via TerminologyRouter and select with MedGemma.
     2→
     3→This node is the fourth in the protocol processing pipeline:
     4→ingest→extract→parse→ground→persist
     5→It implements the delegation pattern: thin orchestration that calls tools.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Error accumulation: entity failures are logged and accumulated, not fatal
     9→- Audit trail: all grounding decisions logged to AuditLog
    10→- TerminologyRouter handles entity-type-aware API routing
    11→- MedGemma selects the best match from candidates
    12→- Field mappings generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition
    13→- Consent entities skipped explicitly (not groundable to medical codes)
    14→- Demographics routed normally via umls/snomed (age/gender agentic reasoning)
    15→- Agentic retry loop: max 3 attempts, expert_review routing on exhaustion
    16→
    17→Architecture note: Graph nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import asyncio
    23→import json
    24→import logging
    25→import os
    26→import re
    27→import time
    28→from typing import Any
    29→
    30→from api_service.storage import engine
    31→from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    32→from shared.models import AuditLog
    33→from sqlmodel import Session
    34→
    35→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    36→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    37→from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import generate_field_mappings
    38→from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
    39→    agentic_reasoning_loop,
    40→    medgemma_decide,
    41→)
    42→from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import (
    43→    OmopLookupResult,
    44→    _get_omop_engine,
    45→    lookup_omop_concept,
    46→)
    47→from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import (
    48→    TerminologyRouter,
    49→    _is_likely_acronym,
    50→)
    51→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import normalize_value
    52→
    53→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    54→
    55→# Singleton router instance (loads YAML config once)
    56→_router: TerminologyRouter | None = None
    57→
    58→
    59→def _get_router() -> TerminologyRouter:
    60→    """Get or create the singleton TerminologyRouter."""
    61→    global _router  # noqa: PLW0603
    62→    if _router is None:
    63→        _router = TerminologyRouter()
    64→    return _router
    65→
    66→
    67→def _reconcile_dual_grounding(
    68→    result: EntityGroundingResult,
    69→    omop_result: OmopLookupResult,
    70→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    71→    """Reconcile TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper results.
    72→
    73→    Sets omop_concept_id and reconciliation_status on the result:
    74→    - Both succeed → "agree" (high confidence) or "disagreement"
    75→    - Only TerminologyRouter → "omop_missing"
    76→    - Only OMOP → "tooluniverse_missing"
    77→    - Neither → no change (existing expert_review behavior)
    78→
    79→    Agreement is checked by comparing the OMOP concept_name against
    80→    the ToolUniverse preferred_term (fuzzy match).
    81→
    82→    Args:
    83→        result: EntityGroundingResult from TerminologyRouter path.
    84→        omop_result: OmopLookupResult from OMOP mapper path.
    85→
    86→    Returns:
    87→        The same EntityGroundingResult, mutated with OMOP fields.
    88→    """
    89→    tu_ok = result.selected_code is not None
    90→    omop_ok = omop_result.omop_concept_id is not None
    91→
    92→    if tu_ok and omop_ok:
    93→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
    94→        # Fuzzy agreement check using token_set_ratio (order-insensitive)
    95→        tu_term = (result.preferred_term or "").lower()
    96→        omop_term = (omop_result.omop_concept_name or "").lower()
    97→        if tu_term and omop_term and fuzz.token_set_ratio(tu_term, omop_term) >= 80:
    98→            result.reconciliation_status = "agree"
    99→        else:
   100→            result.reconciliation_status = "disagreement"
   101→        logger.info(
   102→            "Dual grounding for '%s': TU=%s, OMOP=%s, status=%s",
   103→            result.entity_text[:50],
   104→            result.selected_code,
   105→            omop_result.omop_concept_id,
   106→            result.reconciliation_status,
   107→        )
   108→    elif tu_ok and not omop_ok:
   109→        result.reconciliation_status = "omop_missing"
   110→    elif not tu_ok and omop_ok:
   111→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
   112→        result.reconciliation_status = "tooluniverse_missing"
   113→    # else: neither — leave as-is (expert_review path)
   114→
   115→    return result
   116→
   117→
   118→def _log_grounding_audit(
   119→    session: Session,
   120→    protocol_id: str,
   121→    criterion_id: str,
   122→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   123→    candidates: list[Any],
   124→    result: Any,
   125→) -> None:
   126→    """Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.
   127→
   128→    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,
   129→    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:
   130→    "All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged."
   131→
   132→    Args:
   133→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   134→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   135→        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.
   136→        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.
   137→        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   138→        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.
   139→    """
   140→    audit = AuditLog(
   141→        event_type="entity_grounded",
   142→        target_type="criteria",
   143→        target_id=criterion_id,
   144→        details={
   145→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   146→            "entity_text": result.entity_text,
   147→            "entity_type": result.entity_type,
   148→            "candidate_count": len(candidates),
   149→            "candidates": [
   150→                {
   151→                    "source_api": c.source_api,
   152→                    "code": c.code,
   153→                    "preferred_term": c.preferred_term,
   154→                    "score": c.score,
   155→                }
   156→                for c in candidates
   157→            ],
   158→            "selected_code": result.selected_code,
   159→            "selected_system": result.selected_system,
   160→            "preferred_term": result.preferred_term,
   161→            "confidence": result.confidence,
   162→            "reasoning": result.reasoning,
   163→            "omop_concept_id": result.omop_concept_id,
   164→            "reconciliation_status": result.reconciliation_status,
   165→        },
   166→    )
   167→    session.add(audit)
   168→
   169→
   170→def _flush_audit_logs(
   171→    protocol_id: str,
   172→    pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]],
   173→) -> None:
   174→    """Batch-write audit log entries in a single session.
   175→
   176→    Args:
   177→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   178→        pending_audits: List of (criterion_id, entity, candidates, result) tuples.
   179→    """
   180→    if not pending_audits:
   181→        return
   182→    try:
   183→        with Session(engine) as session:
   184→            for criterion_id, entity, candidates, result in pending_audits:
   185→                _log_grounding_audit(
   186→                    session,
   187→                    protocol_id,
   188→                    criterion_id,
   189→                    entity,
   190→                    candidates,
   191→                    result,
   192→                )
   193→            session.commit()
   194→    except Exception as audit_error:
   195→        logger.warning(
   196→            "Failed to batch-write %d AuditLog entries: %s",
   197→            len(pending_audits),
   198→            audit_error,
   199→        )
   200→
   201→
   202→async def _ground_entity_with_retry(
   203→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   204→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   205→    criterion_text: str,
   206→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult, int]:
   207→    """Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).
   208→
   209→    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:
   210→    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma
   211→    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
   212→    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)
   213→    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review
   214→
   215→    Args:
   216→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   217→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   218→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   219→
   220→    Returns:
   221→        Tuple of (EntityGroundingResult, attempt_count). May have expert_review
   222→        marker in reasoning.
   223→    """
   224→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   225→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   226→
   227→    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates
   228→    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
   229→
   230→    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty
   231→    apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   232→    if not candidates:
   233→        if not apis:
   234→            logger.info(
   235→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter"
   236→                " — no APIs configured for this type",
   237→                entity_text[:50],
   238→                entity_type,
   239→            )
   240→            # No APIs configured → route directly to expert_review
   241→            return (
   242→                EntityGroundingResult(
   243→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   244→                    entity_type=entity_type,
   245→                    selected_code=None,
   246→                    selected_system=None,
   247→                    preferred_term=None,
   248→                    confidence=0.0,
   249→                    candidates=[],
   250→                    reasoning=(
   251→                        "[expert_review] No terminology APIs configured "
   252→                        f"for entity type '{entity_type}'. "
   253→                        "Routed to expert review."
   254→                    ),
   255→                ),
   256→                1,
   257→            )
   258→        else:
   259→            logger.info(
   260→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs",
   261→                entity_text[:50],
   262→                entity_type,
   263→            )
   264→
   265→    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates
   266→    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
   267→
   268→    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total
   269→    attempt = 1
   270→    current_query = entity_text
   271→    while (
   272→        result.selected_code is None
   273→        and result.confidence < 0.5
   274→        and attempt < 3
   275→        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   276→    ):
   277→        attempt += 1
   278→        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(
   279→            entity={**entity, "_previous_query": current_query},
   280→            criterion_context=criterion_text,
   281→            router=router,
   282→            attempt=attempt,
   283→        )
   284→
   285→        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
   286→            logger.info(
   287→                "Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning"
   288→                " (attempt %d) — skipping",
   289→                entity_text[:50],
   290→                attempt,
   291→            )
   292→            return (
   293→                EntityGroundingResult(
   294→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   295→                    entity_type=entity_type,
   296→                    selected_code=None,
   297→                    selected_system=None,
   298→                    preferred_term=None,
   299→                    confidence=0.0,
   300→                    candidates=[],
   301→                    reasoning=(
   302→                        f"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): "
   303→                        f"{reasoning_result.reasoning}"
   304→                    ),
   305→                ),
   306→                attempt,
   307→            )
   308→
   309→        new_query = (
   310→            reasoning_result.rephrased_query
   311→            or reasoning_result.derived_term
   312→            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion
   313→            or current_query
   314→        )
   315→        current_query = new_query
   316→
   317→        logger.info(
   318→            "Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'",
   319→            attempt,
   320→            entity_text[:50],
   321→            new_query[:50],
   322→        )
   323→
   324→        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
   325→        if new_candidates:
   326→            retry_entity = {**entity, "text": new_query}
   327→            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)
   328→            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
   329→                # Success — preserve original entity_text
   330→                return (
   331→                    EntityGroundingResult(
   332→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   333→                        entity_type=result.entity_type,
   334→                        selected_code=result.selected_code,
   335→                        selected_system=result.selected_system,
   336→                        preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   337→                        confidence=result.confidence,
   338→                        candidates=result.candidates,
   339→                        reasoning=(
   340→                            f"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] "
   341→                            f"{result.reasoning}"
   342→                        ),
   343→                        field_mappings=result.field_mappings,
   344→                    ),
   345→                    attempt,
   346→                )
   347→
   348→    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success
   349→    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
   350→        logger.warning(
   351→            "Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after"
   352→            " %d failed grounding attempts",
   353→            entity_text[:50],
   354→            entity_type,
   355→            attempt,
   356→        )
   357→        return (
   358→            EntityGroundingResult(
   359→                entity_text=result.entity_text,
   360→                entity_type=result.entity_type,
   361→                selected_code=result.selected_code,
   362→                selected_system=result.selected_system,
   363→                preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   364→                confidence=result.confidence,
   365→                candidates=result.candidates,
   366→                reasoning=(
   367→                    f"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after "
   368→                    f"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. "
   369→                    f"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}"
   370→                ),
   371→            ),
   372→            attempt,
   373→        )
   374→
   375→    return (result, attempt)
   376→
   377→
   378→def _ground_categorical_values(
   379→    mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   380→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   381→    """Ground categorical values in field mappings using unit_normalizer.
   382→
   383→    For standard-type mappings with non-numeric values (e.g. "Severe",
   384→    "positive", "NYHA Class III"), attempt to resolve a value_concept_id
   385→    using the unit_normalizer's value lookup.
   386→
   387→    This is a best-effort post-processing step — failures don't block
   388→    the grounding result.
   389→
   390→    Args:
   391→        mappings: List of field mapping dicts from generate_field_mappings.
   392→
   393→    Returns:
   394→        The same list with value_concept_id populated where possible.
   395→    """
   396→    numeric_re = re.compile(r"^[\d.,\-]+$")
   397→
   398→    for mapping in mappings:
   399→        try:
   400→            value_obj = mapping.get("value", {})
   401→            if not isinstance(value_obj, dict):
   402→                continue
   403→
   404→            # Only process standard-type values
   405→            if value_obj.get("type") != "standard":
   406→                continue
   407→
   408→            val_text = value_obj.get("value")
   409→            if not val_text or not isinstance(val_text, str):
   410→                continue
   411→
   412→            # Skip numeric values — they don't need concept grounding
   413→            if numeric_re.match(val_text.strip()):
   414→                continue
   415→
   416→            # Already grounded
   417→            if mapping.get("value_concept_id"):
   418→                continue
   419→
   420→            # Try unit_normalizer's categorical value lookup (fast path)
   421→            normalized_text, concept_id = normalize_value(val_text)
   422→            if concept_id is not None:
   423→                mapping["value_concept_id"] = str(concept_id)
   424→                mapping["value_concept_system"] = "OMOP"
   425→                logger.debug(
   426→                    "Grounded categorical value '%s' → concept_id=%s",
   427→                    val_text,
   428→                    concept_id,
   429→                )
   430→        except Exception as e:
   431→            logger.debug("Categorical value grounding failed for mapping: %s", e)
   432→
   433→    return mappings
   434→
   435→
   436→async def _ground_entity_parallel(
   437→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   438→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   439→    criterion_text: str,
   440→    entity_num: int,
   441→    total: int,
   442→    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
   443→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None, float, int]:
   444→    """Ground a single entity with concurrency control and timing.
   445→
   446→    Acquires the semaphore before grounding to cap concurrent API calls.
   447→    Returns a (result, error) tuple for error isolation -- one entity failure
   448→    does not crash others in the asyncio.gather batch.
   449→
   450→    Args:
   451→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   452→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   453→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   454→        entity_num: 1-based index for logging.
   455→        total: Total entity count for logging.
   456→        semaphore: Concurrency limiter (Semaphore(4)).
   457→
   458→    Returns:
   459→        (EntityGroundingResult, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts) on success,
   460→        or (None, error_message, elapsed_ms, 0) on failure.
   461→    """
   462→    async with semaphore:
   463→        entity_text = entity.get("text", "")[:50]
   464→        entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   465→        start = time.monotonic()
   466→        logger.info(
   467→            "Grounding entity %d/%d: '%s' (type=%s) — start",
   468→            entity_num,
   469→            total,
   470→            entity_text,
   471→            entity_type,
   472→        )
   473→        try:
   474→            # Dual grounding: run TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper
   475→            # in parallel per entity (Phase 1a)
   476→            full_entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   477→            acronym = _is_likely_acronym(full_entity_text)
   478→            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
   479→            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(
   480→                full_entity_text, entity_type, is_acronym=acronym
   481→            )
   482→            (result, retry_attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(
   483→                tu_task, omop_task
   484→            )
   485→
   486→            # Reconcile dual grounding results
   487→            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
   488→
   489→            # Thread criterion_id from parse into result
   490→            criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id")
   491→            if criterion_id:
   492→                result.criterion_id = criterion_id
   493→
   494→            # Generate field mappings (same semaphore slot)
   495→            field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(result, criterion_text)
   496→
   497→            # Post-process: ground categorical values in field mappings
   498→            if field_mappings:
   499→                field_mappings = _ground_categorical_values(field_mappings)
   500→
   501→            result.field_mappings = field_mappings if field_mappings else None
   502→
   503→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   504→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   505→            logger.info(
   506→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' grounded in %.1fs: code=%s, omop=%s, conf=%.2f",
   507→                entity_num,
   508→                total,
   509→                entity_text,
   510→                elapsed,
   511→                result.selected_code or "N/A",
   512→                result.omop_concept_id or "N/A",
   513→                result.confidence,
   514→            )
   515→            return (result, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts)
   516→        except Exception as e:
   517→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   518→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   519→            logger.error(
   520→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' failed in %.1fs: %s",
   521→                entity_num,
   522→                total,
   523→                entity_text,
   524→                elapsed,
   525→                e,
   526→            )
   527→            return (None, str(e), elapsed_ms, 0)
   528→
   529→
   530→async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   531→    """Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.
   532→
   533→    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   534→    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for
   535→    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded
   536→    entity.
   537→
   538→    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in
   539→    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total
   540→    failure to parse entities_json triggers a fatal error.
   541→
   542→    Args:
   543→        state: Current pipeline state with entities_json and protocol_id.
   544→
   545→    Returns:
   546→        Dict with grounded_entities_json and accumulated errors list,
   547→        or fatal error dict if entities_json cannot be parsed.
   548→    """
   549→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
   550→
   551→    if state.get("error"):
   552→        return {}
   553→
   554→    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
   555→    with pipeline_span(
   556→        "ground_node", span_type="TOOL", protocol_id=protocol_id
   557→    ) as span:
   558→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
   559→
   560→        try:
   561→            entities_json = state.get("entities_json")
   562→            if not entities_json:
   563→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No entities_json in state"})
   564→                return {
   565→                    "error": "No entities_json in state — parse node may have failed",
   566→                }
   567→
   568→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = json.loads(entities_json)
   569→            protocol_id = state["protocol_id"]
   570→
   571→            span.set_inputs(
   572→                {
   573→                    "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   574→                    "entity_count": len(entity_items),
   575→                }
   576→            )
   577→
   578→            logger.info(
   579→                "Starting grounding for protocol %s: %d entities",
   580→                protocol_id,
   581→                len(entity_items),
   582→            )
   583→
   584→            # Fail fast: verify OMOP vocabulary is reachable before
   585→            # starting entity grounding. No silent fallback.
   586→            _get_omop_engine()
   587→
   588→            router = _get_router()
   589→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
   590→            grounding_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   591→
   592→            # Pre-process: handle consent entities synchronously (skip grounding)
   593→            entities_to_ground: list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]] = []
   594→            for idx, entity in enumerate(entity_items, 1):
   595→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   596→                entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   597→
   598→                if entity_type == "Consent":
   599→                    logger.info(
   600→                        "Entity '%s' (type=Consent) skipped — consent/participation"
   601→                        " criteria are not groundable to terminology codes",
   602→                        entity_text[:50],
   603→                    )
   604→                    consent_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   605→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   606→                        entity_type=entity_type,
   607→                        selected_code=None,
   608→                        selected_system=None,
   609→                        preferred_term=None,
   610→                        confidence=0.0,
   611→                        candidates=[],
   612→                        reasoning=(
   613→                            "Consent/participation criterion — not groundable"
   614→                            " to medical terminology codes"
   615→                        ),
   616→                        criterion_id=entity.get("criterion_id"),
   617→                    )
   618→                    grounding_results.append(consent_result.model_dump())
   619→                else:
   620→                    entities_to_ground.append((idx, entity))
   621→
   622→            # Dev/debug: truncate entity list for faster runs and fewer API calls.
   623→            # DEV_MAX_ENTITIES takes precedence over PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES when set.
   624→            _max_entities = int(
   625→                os.getenv("DEV_MAX_ENTITIES")
   626→                or os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES")
   627→                or "0"
   628→            )
   629→            if _max_entities > 0:
   630→                entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]
   631→                logger.info(
   632→                    "Max entities limit=%d: truncated to %d entities",
   633→                    _max_entities,
   634→                    len(entities_to_ground),
   635→                )
   636→
   637→            # Pre-flight warmup: absorb MedGemma cold-start latency before
   638→            # the parallel batch. Non-fatal — if warmup fails, entities proceed.
   639→            try:
   640→                from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage as HumanMessageType
   641→
   642→                from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
   643→                    _get_medgemma_model,
   644→                )
   645→
   646→                warmup_model = _get_medgemma_model()
   647→                await warmup_model.ainvoke([HumanMessageType(content="ready")])
   648→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup succeeded")
   649→            except Exception as warmup_err:
   650→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup skipped: %s", warmup_err)
   651→
   652→            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore
   653→            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop
   654→            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
   655→
   656→            tasks = [
   657→                _ground_entity_parallel(
   658→                    entity,
   659→                    router,
   660→                    entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", ""),
   661→                    idx,
   662→                    len(entity_items),
   663→                    semaphore,
   664→                )
   665→                for idx, entity in entities_to_ground
   666→            ]
   667→
   668→            outcomes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
   669→
   670→            # Process outcomes and collect audit entries for batch write
   671→            pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]] = []
   672→            entity_times: list[float] = []
   673→            total_retry_count = 0
   674→            for (idx, entity), (result, error, elapsed_ms, retries) in zip(
   675→                entities_to_ground, outcomes
   676→            ):
   677→                entity_times.append(elapsed_ms)
   678→                total_retry_count += max(0, retries - 1)  # retries beyond first attempt
   679→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   680→                criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   681→
   682→                if result is not None:
   683→                    pending_audits.append(
   684→                        (criterion_id, entity, result.candidates, result)
   685→                    )
   686→                    grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())
   687→                else:
   688→                    # Entity failed — create expert_review fallback
   689→                    cid_short = criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else "unknown"
   690→                    error_msg = (
   691→                        f"Entity grounding failed for '{entity_text[:50]}'"
   692→                        f" (criterion={cid_short}): {error}"
   693→                    )
   694→                    logger.error(error_msg)
   695→                    accumulated_errors.append(error_msg)
   696→
   697→            # Batch-write all audit log entries in a single session
   698→            _flush_audit_logs(protocol_id, pending_audits)
   699→
   700→            logger.info(
   701→                "Ground node complete for protocol %s: %d grounded, %d errors",
   702→                protocol_id,
   703→                len(grounding_results),
   704→                len(accumulated_errors),
   705→            )
   706→
   707→            span.set_outputs(
   708→                {
   709→                    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   710→                    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   711→                    "avg_entity_ms": (
   712→                        round(sum(entity_times) / len(entity_times))
   713→                        if entity_times
   714→                        else 0
   715→                    ),
   716→                    "max_entity_ms": round(max(entity_times)) if entity_times else 0,
   717→                    "retry_count": total_retry_count,
   718→                }
   719→            )
   720→
   721→            return {
   722→                "grounded_entities_json": json.dumps(grounding_results),
   723→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   724→            }
   725→
   726→        except Exception as e:
   727→            logger.exception(
   728→                "Ground node fatal error for protocol %s: %s",
   729→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   730→                e,
   731→            )
   732→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   733→            return {"error": f"Ground node failed: {e}"}
   734→

     1→"""TerminologyRouter: entity-type-aware routing to terminology APIs via ToolUniverse.
     2→
     3→Routes entities to terminology APIs based on entity type using a YAML config.
     4→All 6 systems (UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10, LOINC, RxNorm, HPO) are accessed via the
     5→ToolUniverse SDK singleton in tooluniverse_client.py.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Entity type → API mapping stored in config file (YAML), not hardcoded.
     9→- All terminology systems accessed via ToolUniverse SDK (single dependency).
    10→- On API failure: retry transient errors; continue on permanent failures.
    11→- Consent entities explicitly skipped with logging.
    12→- Demographic entities routed normally (not skipped); MedGemma handles derived
    13→  entity mapping (age → birthDate concept, gender → SNOMED gender concepts).
    14→
    15→See config/routing.yaml for the entity type → API mapping.
    16→"""
    17→
    18→from __future__ import annotations
    19→
    20→import logging
    21→from pathlib import Path
    22→
    23→import yaml
    24→from platformdirs import user_cache_dir
    25→from tenacity import (
    26→    before_sleep_log,
    27→    retry,
    28→    retry_if_exception_type,
    29→    stop_after_attempt,
    30→    wait_random_exponential,
    31→)

    34→
    35→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    36→
    37→# Default routing config path relative to this file
    38→_DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config" / "routing.yaml"

    41→def _is_likely_acronym(text: str) -> bool:
    42→    """Check if text is likely a medical acronym.
    43→
    44→    Returns True if the text is short (<=6 chars), all uppercase, and
    45→    contains no spaces. No allowlist — adding UMLS redundantly for
    46→    terms like "BMI" or "AST" costs one extra API call but never breaks
    47→    anything, while maintaining an allowlist is brittle.
    48→
    49→    Pure string check — zero cost.
    50→
    51→    Args:
    52→        text: The entity text to check.
    53→
    54→    Returns:
    55→        True if the text appears to be a medical acronym.
    56→    """
    57→    stripped = text.strip()
    58→    if not stripped or " " in stripped:
    59→        return False
    60→    if len(stripped) > 6:
    61→        return False
    62→    return stripped.upper() == stripped
    63→
    64→
    65→# diskcache TTL: 7 days in seconds
    66→_CACHE_TTL = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60
    67→
    68→
    69→def _get_cache():
    70→    """Get or create the diskcache Cache instance.
    71→
    72→    Returns:
    73→        diskcache.Cache instance stored in user cache dir, or None if
    74→        diskcache is not installed.
    75→    """
    76→    try:
    77→        import diskcache
    78→
    79→        cache_dir = Path(user_cache_dir("medgemma-terminology-router"))
    80→        return diskcache.Cache(str(cache_dir))
    81→    except ImportError:
    82→        logger.debug("diskcache not installed — terminology API caching disabled.")
    83→        return None
    84→
    85→
    86→class TransientAPIError(Exception):
    87→    """Retriable API failure: rate limit (429), timeout (408), server error (5xx).
    88→
    89→    TerminologyRouter retries these automatically with exponential backoff.
    90→    """
    91→
    92→
    93→class PermanentAPIError(Exception):
    94→    """Non-retriable API failure: auth error (401/403), validation (400/422).
    95→
    96→    TerminologyRouter logs and skips these — retrying cannot fix them.
    97→    """
    98→
    99→
   100→class TerminologyRouter:
   101→    """Route entities to terminology APIs via ToolUniverse SDK.
   102→
   103→    Loads entity type → API mapping from routing.yaml. For each entity,
   104→    queries all matching APIs via the ToolUniverse singleton client and
   105→    returns a list of GroundingCandidate objects. MedGemma then selects
   106→    the best match downstream.
   107→
   108→    Implements error accumulation: individual API failures are logged and
   109→    skipped, not propagated. The entity is still grounded by remaining APIs.
   110→
   111→    Usage:
   112→        router = TerminologyRouter()
   113→        candidates = await router.route_entity("metformin", "Medication")
   114→        # candidates: [GroundingCandidate(source_api="rxnorm", ...), ...]
   115→
   116→    Attributes:
   117→        config: Parsed YAML config dict.
   118→        config_path: Path to the routing.yaml file used.
   119→    """
   120→
   121→    def __init__(self, config_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   122→        """Initialize TerminologyRouter with routing config.
   123→
   124→        Args:
   125→            config_path: Path to routing.yaml. Defaults to config/routing.yaml
   126→                relative to this package.
   127→        """
   128→        self.config_path = config_path or _DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH
   129→        with open(self.config_path) as f:
   130→            self.config: dict = yaml.safe_load(f)
   131→
   132→    def get_apis_for_entity(self, entity_type: str) -> list[str]:
   133→        """Get list of API names to query for this entity type.
   134→
   135→        Returns an empty list for entity types with skip: true in routing config
   136→        (e.g. Consent) and for entity types not found in the routing config.
   137→        Both cases are logged.
   138→
   139→        Args:
   140→            entity_type: Entity type string (e.g. "Medication", "Condition").
   141→
   142→        Returns:
   143→            List of API names to query, or empty list if entity should be skipped.
   144→        """
   145→        routing_rules: dict = self.config.get("routing_rules", {})
   146→        rule = routing_rules.get(entity_type)
   147→
   148→        if rule is None:
   149→            logger.warning(
   150→                "Unknown entity type '%s' — not in routing config. "
   151→                "Skipping grounding. Add to config/routing.yaml to enable.",
   152→                entity_type,
   153→            )
   154→            return []
   155→
   156→        # Explicit skip (e.g. Consent: {skip: true})
   157→        if isinstance(rule, dict) and rule.get("skip"):
   158→            logger.info(
   159→                "Entity type '%s' is explicitly skipped in routing config "
   160→                "(no terminology grounding for this type).",
   161→                entity_type,
   162→            )
   163→            return []
   164→
   165→        if isinstance(rule, list):
   166→            return rule
   167→
   168→        # Unexpected rule format — treat as unknown
   169→        logger.warning(
   170→            "Unexpected routing rule format for '%s': %r. Skipping.",
   171→            entity_type,
   172→            rule,
   173→        )
   174→        return []
   175→
   176→    async def route_entity(
   177→        self, entity_text: str, entity_type: str
   178→    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   179→        """Route entity to all matching APIs and return all candidates.
   180→
   181→        Queries each API configured for the entity type via ToolUniverse SDK.
   182→        Individual API failures are logged and skipped (error accumulation).
   183→        The caller (MedGemma ground node) receives all available candidates
   184→        and selects the best match.
   185→
   186→        For likely acronyms, UMLS is always included in the API list
   187→        regardless of entity type configuration (UMLS has the broadest
   188→        abbreviation coverage via AUI atom-level synonyms).
   189→
   190→        Args:
   191→            entity_text: The entity text to look up (e.g. "metformin").
   192→            entity_type: The entity type (e.g. "Medication").
   193→
   194→        Returns:
   195→            List of GroundingCandidate objects from all queried APIs.
   196→            Empty list if entity type is skipped or all APIs fail.
   197→        """
   198→        api_names = self.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   199→
   200→        if not api_names:
   201→            return []
   202→
   203→        # Smart acronym routing: always include UMLS for acronyms
   204→        if _is_likely_acronym(entity_text) and "umls" not in api_names:
   205→            api_names = [*api_names, "umls"]
   206→            logger.info(
   207→                "Acronym detected ('%s') — adding UMLS to routing "
   208→                "for broader abbreviation coverage",
   209→                entity_text,
   210→            )
   211→
   212→        all_candidates: list[GroundingCandidate] = []
   213→
   214→        for api_name in api_names:
   215→            try:
   216→                candidates = await self._query_tooluniverse(api_name, entity_text)
   217→                all_candidates.extend(candidates)
   218→
   219→            except PermanentAPIError as e:
   220→                # Non-retriable: log and skip this API
   221→                logger.error(
   222→                    "Permanent API error from '%s' for entity '%s': %s",
   223→                    api_name,
   224→                    entity_text,
   225→                    e,
   226→                )
   227→            except Exception as e:
   228→                # Transient/unexpected: log and skip (tenacity already retried)
   229→                logger.error(
   230→                    "API '%s' failed for entity '%s' after retries: %s",
   231→                    api_name,
   232→                    entity_text,
   233→                    e,
   234→                )
   235→
   236→        return all_candidates
   237→
   238→    @retry(
   239→        retry=retry_if_exception_type(TransientAPIError),
   240→        stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
   241→        wait=wait_random_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),
   242→        before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
   243→        reraise=True,
   244→    )
   245→    async def _query_tooluniverse(
   246→        self, api_name: str, entity_text: str
   247→    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   248→        """Query terminology system via ToolUniverse client wrapper.
   249→
   250→        Uses the singleton search_terminology function which handles tool
   251→        dispatch, response parsing, and in-memory TTL caching.
   252→
   253→        Retries on TransientAPIError with exponential backoff (3 attempts max).
   254→
   255→        Args:
   256→            api_name: Terminology system name (e.g. "umls", "icd10").
   257→            entity_text: Term to search.
   258→
   259→        Returns:
   260→            List of GroundingCandidate objects from ToolUniverse.
   261→
   262→        Raises:
   263→            TransientAPIError: On retriable errors (rate limit, timeout, 5xx).
   264→        """
   265→        from protocol_processor.tools.tooluniverse_client import search_terminology
   266→
   267→        candidates = await search_terminology(api_name, entity_text, max_results=10)
   268→        return candidates
   269→
```

     1→You are a medical terminology expert. Your task is to select the best terminology code for a medical entity extracted from a clinical trial eligibility criterion.
     2→
     3→You will be given:
     4→- The entity text and type
     5→- The criterion context it came from
     6→- A list of terminology candidates from medical APIs (UMLS, SNOMED, RxNorm, etc.)
     7→
     8→Your job: Select the single best match or determine no match exists.
     9→
    10→## Confidence Scoring Guidelines
    11→
    12→- **0.9-1.0**: Exact match — entity text maps directly to the concept's preferred term or is a recognized synonym
    13→- **0.7-0.8**: Close synonym — entity is a common medical synonym (e.g., "heart attack" → "myocardial infarction")
    14→- **0.5-0.6**: Partial/broader match — concept is related but broader or narrower than the entity
    15→- **0.0**: No valid match or entity is not a meaningful medical concept
    16→
    17→## Rules
    18→
    19→1. Prefer exact or near-exact concept matches over broad categories
    20→2. For medications, prefer RxNorm over UMLS when both are available
    21→3. For conditions and procedures, UMLS or SNOMED is appropriate
    22→4. If the entity is a number, unit, or procedural language (not a medical concept), set confidence=0.0
    23→5. Always provide reasoning for your selection
    24→6. Output your analysis in plain text — the output will be structured by a downstream system
    25→
    26→## Search Strategy (for retry attempts)
    27→
    28→If your first terminology search yields 0 results:
    29→- DO NOT retry the exact same phrase or minor word-order variations.
    30→- Simplify to the core medical noun: "Left ventricular hypertrophy" → "ventricular hypertrophy" → "hypertrophy".
    31→- Drop anatomical qualifiers first, then adjectives: "bilateral chronic kidney disease" → "chronic kidney disease" → "kidney disease".
    32→- Expand abbreviations: "HTN" → "hypertension", "MI" → "myocardial infarction", "DM" → "diabetes mellitus", "CKD" → "chronic kidney disease", "COPD" → "chronic obstructive pulmonary disease".
    33→- Try the generic drug class if a specific brand name fails: "Keytruda" → "pembrolizumab" → "anti-PD-1 monoclonal antibody".
    34→- Each retry MUST use a substantially different query from all previous attempts.
    35→
